### [1] [STATERA: Hidden Mass Estimation via Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Kinematics using Frozen Temporal Tubelets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00003)
*Animesh Varma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究如何从单目视频中预测不透明非对称刚体质心的隐藏物理属性，提出STATERA方法结合预训练视频 backbone和轻量级时序混合器，在HiddenMass基准上实现从模拟到真实零样本迁移，物理捕获率从2.6%提升至41.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模型在帧级外观预训练后，难以从运动中推断隐藏的物理属性。在自遮挡情况下，表面线索和点追踪不可靠，因此需要研究如何从短单目视频中定位非对称刚体的质心（CoM）。

Method: 提出STATERA方法：1）使用预训练视频 backbone（V-JEPA），保持大部分权重冻结；2）添加轻量级时序 tubelet 混合器；3）预测每帧 CoM 热图和轨迹。构建 HiddenMass 基准：50K MuJoCo 模拟轨迹 + 63个序列的真实测试集（含物理校准的 CoM 真值）。

Result: 模拟实验中，STATERA-50K-Sigma 将归一化 CoM 误差从 41.7%（DINOv2）降至 25.2%。零样本 sim-to-real 迁移中发现监督目标的权衡：相位感知目标可能诱导双峰预测，相位无关目标可能坍缩向统计安全质心。STATERA-50K-Crescent 是唯一展示朝向真实隐藏偏移量一致移动的方法，虽存在单目向量 overshoot 伪影导致绝对欧氏误差略增，但物理捕获率从 2.6% 提升至 41.0%。

Conclusion: 冻结的时序表示可以更好地区分惯性动力学与视觉几何，从而实现隐藏参数估计。这一方法为从视觉信号中推断物理隐藏属性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision models pretrained for frame-level appearance often struggle to infer hidden physical properties from motion. We study center-of-mass (CoM) localization for opaque, asymmetric rigid bodies from short monocular videos, where surface cues and point tracking are unreliable under self-occlusion. We propose STATERA, which adapts a pretrained video backbone (V-JEPA) with mostly frozen weights and a lightweight temporal tubelet mixer to predict per-frame CoM heatmaps and trajectories. To support this task, we introduce the HiddenMass Benchmark, comprising 50K MuJoCo trajectories and a 63-sequence real-world test set with physically calibrated CoM ground truth. In simulation, STATERA-50K-Sigma improves normalized CoM error from 41.7% (DINOv2) to 25.2%. In zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, we observe a fundamental trade-off in supervision: phase-aware targets can induce bimodal predictions, while phase-agnostic targets can collapse toward statistically safe centroids. Nevertheless, our phase-aware STATERA-50K-Crescent is the only evaluated method that demonstrates consistent movement toward the true hidden offset. While this leads to a monocular vector overshoot artifact that marginally increases absolute Euclidean error compared to a static geometric centroid, it improves physics capture from 2.6% to 41.0%. These results suggest that frozen temporal representations can better separate inertial dynamics from visual geometry for hidden-parameter estimation.

</details>


### [2] [Emergent Object Binding Has a Finite Spatial Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00006)
*Mayank Singal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 预训练Vision Transformer可以从冻结的patch嵌入中解码出两个图像块是否属于同一物体（IsSameObject信号），这表明物体绑定仅从自监督预训练中就能涌现。该信号具有空间局部性：两个同物体块被解码为绑定的概率随距离单调下降，可以用有限尺度的指数函数描述。这种衰减是表示本身的特性而非解码器所致。局部绑定可解释聚类score无法解释的一系列行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索预训练Vision Transformer中是否涌现出物体绑定能力，以及该信号的结构特征。现有单一准确率指标掩盖了信号的内在结构。

Method: 使用三种探针方法在ADE20K和COCO数据集上对DINO和CLIP骨干网络进行实验，控制混淆变量后映射各种行为。

Result: 绑定具有空间局部性：同物体块绑定概率随距离指数衰减，在非零水平达到平稳。该特性跨对象尺寸、探针类型、数据集和骨干网络均成立。

Conclusion: 物体绑定从自监督预训练中涌现，且表现为具有有限作用范围的局部空间连贯性。这解释了大物体上绑定减弱、同类不同物体分离不可靠、物体部分与整体分组等行为，但与遮挡无关。

Abstract: Pretrained Vision Transformers encode whether two image patches belong to the same object. This IsSameObject signal is decodable from frozen patch embeddings at high accuracy, which suggests that object binding emerges from self-supervised pretraining alone. We show that this single accuracy number hides the structure of the signal. Binding is local: the probability that two patches of the same object are decoded as bound falls off monotonically with the distance between them and levels off at a nonzero floor, a falloff well described by an exponential with a finite length scale. This decay holds across object sizes, across three families of probe, on both ADE20K and COCO, and across DINO and CLIP backbones, which indicates that it is a property of the representation rather than of the decoder. Reading binding as local spatial coherence with a finite range accounts for a set of behaviors that the aggregate score leaves unexplained: binding weakens on large objects, separates distinct objects of the same class less reliably than objects of different classes, and groups object parts with their wholes. It is, by contrast, unaffected by occlusion once object size is controlled. We map each behavior with confounds controlled. As a preliminary observation, the horizon and its floor are organized at different depths in DINOv2 and DINOv3, which we report as suggestive given the small number of layers probed and the confound between the two models.

</details>


### [3] [Spatial Lifting for Dense Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00017)
*Mingzhi Xu,Tao Zhou,Yong Li,Yizhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Spatial Lifting (SL)方法，将2D图像等标准输入升维到高维空间，再用针对该高维设计的网络（如3D U-Net）进行处理，以实现更高效、准确的密集预测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉密集预测任务中，传统方法往往参数量大、推理成本高，作者希望找到一种能够在保持良好性能的同时显著降低模型参数量和推理成本的方法。

Method: Spatial Lifting (SL)通过将标准输入（如2D图像）升维到更高维空间进行处理。该框架在高维空间中产生内在结构化的输出，便于训练时的密集监督，并在测试时实现基于单次前向传播的自洽性质量与不确定性估计。

Result: 在基准任务上，与传统方法相比，SL方法实现了良好的性能，同时显著降低了推理成本和模型参数量。

Conclusion: Spatial Lifting提供了一种简单通用的建模策略，为视觉密集预测任务中更高效、准确、可靠深度网络的发展提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: We present Spatial Lifting (SL), a novel methodology for dense prediction tasks. SL operates by lifting standard inputs, such as 2D images, into a higher-dimensional space and subsequently processing them using networks designed for that higher dimension, such as a 3D U-Net. Counterintuitively, this dimensionality lifting allows us to achieve good performance on benchmark tasks compared to conventional approaches, while reducing inference costs and \textbf{{drastically lowering the number of model parameters}}. The SL framework produces intrinsically structured outputs along the lifted dimension. This emergent structure facilitates dense supervision during training and enables single-forward-pass self-consistency-based quality and uncertainty estimation at test time. Spatial Lifting introduces a simple and general modeling strategy that offers a promising path toward more efficient, accurate, and reliable deep networks for dense prediction tasks in vision.

</details>


### [4] [Encoded but Disconnected: Decomposing Vision-Language Model Failures under a Patching Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00024)
*Genpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究者在三个视觉语言模型架构上验证了中层可解释性的失效：虽然中层编码了正确答案信息，但这些信息对最终预测没有因果贡献。除一个特例外，残差流修补无法改变模型预测。错误可分为三种可学习的失败模式，且架构先验可预测干预效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉语言模型中层是否像之前假设的那样对预测具有因果活性，验证"中层编码答案→影响最终预测"这一假设是否成立。

Method: 在LLaVA-1.5-7B、Qwen2.5-VL-7B、InternVL3-8B上使用POPE基准，通过残差流修补技术测试中层和注意力头对预测的因果影响，并训练分类器识别三种失败模式。

Result: 中层在68-91%的错误中编码了正确答案，但修补实验显示：架构层面零翻转；每头层面Qwen完全无效(0/12,600)；仅InternVL3第20层第2个头有局部效应(p<1e-4)。三种失败模式可在所有架构上以>60%准确率学习。

Conclusion: 中层可解释性是"虚假相关"：编码信息与因果活性分离。错误存在可辨识的操作性分类（感知失败、编码但断开、先验覆盖），这些类别具有机械真实性非虚构，为后续机制研究提供基础。

Abstract: Across three vision-language model architectures (LLaVA-1.5-7B, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, InternVL3-8B), we report a universal negative finding for mid-layer interpretability. On POPE -- the benchmark common to all three -- the mid layers encode the ground-truth answer in 68-91% of errors, yet this signal is not causally active for the final prediction: residual-stream patching yields 0% non-trivial flip at the layer level on all three architectures, and on two of three at the per-head level (Qwen: 0/12,600 patched forwards). The lone exception, InternVL3 layer-20 head-2, is a non-vocab, self-attending head whose effect is localized to that specific head (p < 1e-4). Despite the null, the errors separate operationally into three failure modes -- Perception Failure, Encoded-but-Disconnected, Prior-Override -- learnable above 60% on all three architectures, and the architecture's prior direction predicts which of two interventions elicits a category-specific response. We report these mitigation effects under oracle labels as evidence the categories are mechanistically real, not as a deployable method.

</details>


### [5] [Domain generalization and synthetic data in object detection: the enabler, the probe, and the gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00030)
*Elfi I. S. Hofmeijer,Ella P. Fokkinga,Friso G. Heslinga,Klamer Schutte,Jörgen M. Karlholm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于目标检测领域泛化(DG)的综述论文，探讨了合成数据在应对分布偏移中的三重作用：作为DG的使能器、作为探针分析工具，以及合成到真实域差距的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测模型在部署时常因天气、操作环境或物体外观变化导致性能下降，而现有的域泛化研究专门针对目标检测的较少。合成数据有望支持DG研究，但其在目标检测中的系统性作用尚不明确。

Method: 采用目标检测为中心的DG综述视角，从三个互补维度审视合成数据的作用：1)多样化和对齐策略；2)受控实验探针；3)合成到真实域差距分析。

Result: 识别出现有DG方法在目标检测中的局限性，指出未来研究需要能够同时处理定位和分类的表示感知方法，以应对域偏移问题。

Conclusion: 合成数据在DG中具有重要价值，但现有方法未能充分解决目标检测中定位和分类的联合泛化问题，需要发展更先进的表示感知方法。

Abstract: Object detection models often experience performance degradation when deployed under distribution shifts, caused by for example changes in weather type, operational environment, or object appearance. Domain Generalization (DG) aims to develop models that remain robust under such shifts and generalize well to unseen domains. DG research specifically focused on object detection models is scarce, although these models face additional challenges around localization and multi-scale representations. Synthetic data is a promising tool to support in DG, by enabling large-scale generation of diverse new samples. In this paper, we present an object detection-centric review of DG and examine the role of synthetic data from three complementary perspectives. First, synthetic data acts as an enabler of DG through diversification and alignment strategies that aim to improve robustness to distribution shifts. Second, it serves as a probe that enables controlled experimentation to identify and understand failure modes. Third, we discuss the synthetic-to-real gap, a particularly challenging form of domain shift that arises when models trained on synthetic imagery are deployed on real-world data. Through reviewing these perspectives, we identify limitations of current DG approaches for object detection and argue that future research requires representation-aware methods that explicitly address both localization and classification under domain shift.

</details>


### [6] [Seeing the City or Recognizing the Place? What Street-View Imagery Adds Beyond Existing Urban Data in VLM Urban Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00031)
*Kaizhen Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究比较了街景图像预测与现有城市数据在七个属性上的表现，发现图像对建筑类型、功能和低层楼层计数更有价值，而现有数据在道路损坏、路边坡道和房价方面表现相当或更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 街景图像虽被广泛用于推断城市属性，但仅靠预测准确率无法揭示图像在已有数据基础上的增量价值。研究旨在量化图像相较于现有城市数据的实际贡献。

Method: 对同一城市单元评估图像、任务上下文、邻近观测和公共记录；使用图像替换和冲突记录测试模型对不同来源的依赖程度；对比三种视觉语言模型(VLM)和五个公共数据资源；生成OpenFACADES楼层注释并与OpenStreetMap楼层值对比。

Result: 现有城市数据在道路损坏、路边坡道和房价上与图像模型相当或更优；人口统计接近图像最佳结果；图像在建筑类型、功能和低层楼层计数上信息量更大；楼层计数优势随最近标注建筑距离翻倍增加5.7个百分点；高层建筑因屋顶线超出画面导致优势下降；模型常跟随冲突记录；OpenFACADES显示与纯图像运行相反的高度相关错误模式。

Conclusion: 街景图像价值取决于视觉可读性和本地数据覆盖程度；图像与现有城市数据的对比可为图像采集提供指导，并明确衍生城市地图的数据来源。

Abstract: Street-view imagery is increasingly used to infer urban attributes, but predictive accuracy alone does not reveal how much a photograph contributes beyond data already available for the same place. We compare image-based predictions with existing urban data across seven attributes from five public resources and three VLMs. The same urban units are evaluated using images, task context, nearby observations, and public records, while image replacements and conflicting records test source reliance. Existing urban data matched or exceeded image-only models for road damage, curb ramps, and house price, while neighbouring official statistics nearly matched the best image result for population. Images were more informative for building type, building function, and low-rise floor count. For floor count, image advantage increased by 5.7 percentage points per doubling of distance to the nearest labelled building and declined for tall buildings whose rooflines often fell outside the frame. Models frequently followed conflicting records. OpenFACADES floor annotations were generated with OpenStreetMap floor values and showed the opposite height-dependent error pattern from image-only reruns. Street-view image value therefore depends on visual legibility and local data coverage. Comparing images with existing urban data can guide image collection and clarify the provenance of derived urban maps.

</details>


### [7] [DSSR-3D: Decoupled Reasoning for View-Dependent Referring in 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00040)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Thien-Phuc Tran,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DSSR-3D框架，解决3D Gaussian Splatting中视点相关的引用分割问题。现有方法将语言特征嵌入全局视角不变空间，无法处理依赖于相机姿态的空间关系（如"在...左边"）。该框架通过两个接口实现：视角不变语义定位和视角条件空间推理，无需重新训练底层语义场，也不依赖边界框等离散几何代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D Gaussian Splatting的引用分割方法将语言特征嵌入全局视角不变空间，无法解决依赖相机姿态的观察者中心空间关系（如"在...左边"），这限制了自然语言在3D场景理解中的表达能力和实际应用。

Method: 提出DSSR-3D推理框架，形式化为两个接口：1)视角不变语义定位；2)视角条件空间推理。实例化方式包括：温度锐化softmax定位机制和基于投影的方向评分函数，通过轻量级训练-free融合步骤结合。可零样本迁移到结构不同的语义场。提出ViewRef-GS基准测试，结合增强的Ref-LERF提供全面的视点依赖空间 grounding 测试平台。

Result: 实验表明相比现有3DGS引用方法取得一致性能提升，且无需在基础语义场外进行额外训练。新基准测试ViewRef-GS有效隔离了3D高斯场上的视点相关分割问题。

Conclusion: DSSR-3D框架成功实现了视点相关的3D引用分割，通过解耦语义定位和空间推理两个接口，在无需额外训练的情况下实现了跨场景零样本迁移。为3D场景中自然语言空间关系理解提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting have enabled open-vocabulary and referring segmentation by distilling semantic knowledge from 2D foundation models into 3D representations. However, existing referring fields embed language features in a globally view-invariant space, making them fundamentally unable to resolve observer-centric spatial relations (e.g., "to the left of") that depend on camera pose. We propose DSSR-3D, an inference-time framework for view-dependent referring segmentation on continuous 3D Gaussian fields, formalized as two interfaces - pose-invariant semantic localization and pose-conditioned spatial reasoning - such that any pair of functions satisfying these constraints yields a valid instantiation, requiring no retraining of the underlying semantic field and no reliance on discrete geometric proxies such as bounding boxes. We instantiate the two interfaces with a temperature-sharpened softmax localization mechanism and a projection-based directional scoring function, fused via a lightweight, training-free step, and show they transfer zero-shot to structurally distinct semantic fields without adaptation. We further propose ViewRef-GS, a benchmark isolating view-dependent segmentation on 3D Gaussian fields, evaluated jointly with an augmented Ref-LERF to provide a comprehensive testbed for viewpoint-dependent spatial grounding. Experiments show consistent gains over existing 3DGS-based referring methods, with no additional training beyond the base semantic field

</details>


### [8] [Reachability Is Not Generalization: Understanding Verb--Noun Decomposition in Assembly Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00064)
*Changyi Li,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文系统分析了动词-名词分解在装配动作识别中的组合泛化能力，发现该方法虽突破原子动作分类器的零概率问题，但泛化能力仍受限于训练数据的共现结构，无法实现真正的自由组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有装配系统常遇到已知组件的新组合，原子动作分类器对这些新组合分配零概率。虽然动词-名词分解被广泛采用，但其在组合偏移下的泛化行为尚不清晰，需要系统性分析。

Method: 在MECCANO、HAViD和IMPACT三个装配数据集上对动词-名词分解进行系统性实验分析。

Result: 1) 分解虽突破原子天花板，但泛化仅限于训练数据的密集共现区域；2) 失败主要集中在大词汇量组件；3) 共享编码器引入组件纠缠，导致对共现模式过度依赖，在未见组合上性能下降至多6倍；4) IMPACT数据集的动词密集词汇反转了瓶颈位置。

Conclusion: 组合泛化不完全的根源在于：原始支持不足、词汇不对称导致的瓶颈、以及组件纠缠。提出可移植的诊断框架用于研究组合识别问题。

Abstract: Assembly actions are compositional: they combine a manipulation with a part or tool. In deployment, systems routinely encounter novel combinations of familiar components, yet an atomic action classifier assigns every unseen combination exactly zero probability by construction. The prevailing solution is verb--noun decomposition, which predicts components separately and recombines them to reach unseen actions. While widely adopted, how decomposition generalizes under compositional shift remains poorly understood. We present a systematic analysis of verb--noun decomposition across three assembly datasets (MECCANO, HAViD, and IMPACT). Although decomposition escapes the atomic ceiling, its generalization extends only partially beyond it. Unseen-composition performance remains strongly tied to the co-occurrence structure of the training data, indicating that much of the observed gain arises from interpolation within densely supported regions of the compositional space rather than from unconstrained recombination. Across datasets, failures consistently concentrate on the larger-vocabulary component, and IMPACT's verb-heavy vocabulary reverses the bottleneck from nouns to verbs. We further show that shared-encoder training introduces component entanglement, encouraging reliance on co-occurrence patterns that transfer poorly to unseen compositions and trailing independent recombination by up to $6.0\times$ in harmonic mean. Taken together, these findings explain why decomposition achieves only partial compositional generalization in practice. By identifying primitive support, vocabulary asymmetry, and component entanglement as connected sources of error, we provide a portable diagnostic framework for studying compositional recognition beyond aggregate accuracy. Code: https://github.com/hisalaheiyo/assembly.

</details>


### [9] [Robust Online Aero-Engine Blade Defect Detection via Dual-Alignment Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00067)
*Zhaoyang Wang,Haiyong Chen,Dongying Li,Yining Wang,Huapeng Wu,Xinwei Lv,Atik Shahariar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Aero-engine Blade Defect Detector (ABDD)，一种基于测试时自适应技术的在线缺陷检测框架，用于解决航空发动机叶片制造中的领域偏移问题。该框架通过双对齐策略结合特征统计对齐与伪框对齐，同时利用不确定性感知框过滤机制减少噪声伪标签带来的误差累积，并采用轻量级的稀疏扩张Mona模块实现参数高效增量调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空发动机叶片图像缺陷外观可能随生产线、成像条件、叶片姿态和表面背景变化而变化，导致训练与部署数据之间的领域偏移，深度缺陷检测器的可靠性下降。传统伪标签方法在稀疏缺陷场景下容易受到噪声或缺失预测的影响。

Method: 提出ABDD框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 双对齐策略(Dual-Alignment Strategy)联合适应全局视觉风格和局部缺陷形态，结合特征统计对齐与伪框对齐；2) 不确定性感知框过滤机制(Uncertainty-aware Box Filtering)利用分类置信度、分类熵和定位熵评估伪框可靠性；3) 轻量级Sparse Dilated Mona模块实现参数高效增量调优，同时限制源域遗忘。在CD-AeBD和HD-AeBD数据集上基于RT-DETR + Swin-T架构进行评估。

Result: 实验表明ABDD在多种领域偏移场景下显著提升了检测鲁棒性，并在工业检测平台上得到实际验证。

Conclusion: 所提ABDD框架能有效解决航空发动机叶片缺陷检测中的领域偏移问题，通过双对齐策略和不确定性感知过滤机制提升了在线检测的可靠性，具有重要的工业应用价值。

Abstract: Reliable visual inspection is essential for quality assurance in aero-engine blade manufacturing, where defect appearance may vary across production lines, imaging conditions, blade poses, and surface backgrounds. Such domain shifts cause a mismatch between training and deployment data and degrade the reliability of deep defect detectors in online inspection. This problem is particularly challenging because aero-engine blade images usually contain sparse defects, making pseudolabel-based adaptation vulnerable to noisy or missing predictions. To address this issue, we propose Aero-engine Blade Defect Detector (ABDD), an online adaptive detection framework based on test-time adaptation. ABDD introduces a Dual-Alignment Strategy to jointly adapt global visual style and local defect morphology by combining feature-statistics alignment with pseudo-box alignment. To reduce error accumulation from unreliable pseudo labels, an Uncertainty-aware Box Filtering mechanism evaluates pseudo boxes using classification confidence, classification entropy, and localization entropy. In addition, a lightweight Sparse Dilated Mona module enables parameter-efficient delta tuning while limiting source-domain forgetting. ABDD is evaluated on CD-AeBD and HD-AeBD under multiple domain-shift scenarios, with TTA strategies compared under a unified RT-DETR + Swin-T architecture. Experiments show that ABDD consistently improves detection robustness under domain shifts, and its practicality is further validated on an industrial inspection platform.

</details>


### [10] [Form and Void: Entangled Composition through an Autonomous AI Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02045)
*Shiwen Wang,Jian Yang,Xu Wang,Xincan Wang,Weiming Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaV-A是一个多模态代理，用于分阶段生成正负空间构图。它首先生成基础物体，分析其形状和空间结构以识别负空间语义，最后生成最终图像生成的合成指令，在正负空间构图生成方面优于直接零样本MLLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正负空间是视觉构图的 fundamental 原则，支持视觉连贯的形式和分层语义关系。生成这种构图具有挑战性，因为它需要协调控制共享边界的两个语义概念。现有文本到图像模型和多模态大语言模型在直接单次提示下仍难以实现正负空间生成。

Method: FaV-A遵循渐进式工作流程：(1)首先生成基础物体；(2)分析其形状和空间结构，识别候选负空间语义；(3)为最终图像生成阶段生成合成指令。

Result: 实验结果和消融分析表明，FaV-A在生成视觉连贯且语义对齐的正负空间构图方面比直接零样本MLLM基线更有效。

Conclusion: FaV-A为正负空间构图生成提供了一个更有效的框架，通过渐进式工作流程解决了直接单次提示难以协调正负空间语义的问题。

Abstract: Positive and negative space is a fundamental principle in visual composition, supporting visually coherent forms and layered semantic relationships. Generating such compositions is challenging because it requires coordinated control over two semantic concepts that share a common boundary. Although recent text-to-image models and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance in image generation and visual understanding, positive-negative space generation remains difficult, particularly under direct single-pass prompting. In this work, we present the \textbf{{F}}orm \textbf{{a}}nd \textbf{{V}}oid \textbf{{A}}gent (\textbf{{FaV-A}}), a multimodal agent designed for staged positive-negative space generation. FaV-A follows a progressive workflow: it first generates a base object, then analyzes its shape and spatial structure to identify candidate negative-space semantics, and finally produces compositional instructions for the final image generation stage. Experimental results and ablation analyses suggest that FaV-A provides a more effective framework than direct zero-shot MLLM baselines for producing visually coherent and semantically aligned positive-negative space compositions.

</details>


### [11] [A Framework for Egocentric and Exocentric Procedural Understanding via Temporal Segmentation and Semantic Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00069)
*Vivek Chavan,Jörg Krüger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将长时序的工作场所视频数据转换为紧凑的结构化程序状态记忆（工作环境模型WEM），通过多模态信号检测事件边界，并生成可追溯的事件卡片实现记忆压缩与检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序的自我中心/外部视角视频数据包含丰富的程序性证据，但存在冗余、噪声高、处理成本高和存储成本高的问题，需要一种紧凑的框架来解决这些挑战。

Method: 1) 借鉴事件分割理论，使用视觉上下文、位置、运动、叙述、注视/物体交互及可选的外部视角工作空间证据来检测边界，而非固定窗口或仅依赖视觉新颖性；2) 将每个片段抽象为包含行动者、时间间隔、地点、动作、工具/对象、前后状态、置信度和来源的证据链接事件卡片；3) 使用冻结的DINOv2和VJEPA-2编码器配合本地语言模型实现。

Result: 事件卡片增量更新WEM，实现紧凑、可审计的文档记录和检索，支持本地隐私约束下的长时序问答任务。

Conclusion: 该框架为工作场所视频的紧凑表示和高效检索提供了新思路，评估指标涵盖分割质量、记忆压缩率、检索保真度和长时序问答性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon ego/exo data contains rich procedural evidence, but are redundant, noisy, and costly to process or retain. We propose a compact framework that converts continuous multimodal workplace video into a structured Procedural State Memory, implemented as a Work Environment Model (WEM). Inspired by event segmentation theory, we detect boundaries using changes in visual context, location, motion, narration, gaze/object interaction, and optional exocentric workspace evidence, rather than fixed windows or visual novelty alone. Each segment is abstracted into an evidence-linked event card containing actor, interval, location, action, objects/tools, pre/post state, confidence, and provenance. These event cards incrementally update the WEM, enabling compact, auditable documentation and retrieval under on-premise privacy constraints. We instantiate the design with frozen DINOv2 and VJEPA-2 encoders and a local language model, and outline evaluation criteria for segmentation quality, memory compression, retrieval fidelity, and long-horizon QA.

</details>


### [12] [DramaAgent: Agentic Storytelling Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00097)
*Ting Huang,Biao Wu,Ronghao Chen,Zeyu Zhang,Tengfei Cheng,Qizhen Lan,Huacan Wang,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DramaAgent是一个分层、代理化、模型无关的框架，用于长篇幅文本到视频和音频的生成，通过在视频生成主干网上方引入控制层来改善长篇故事视频的一致性和音画对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到视频生成方法在生成长篇幅故事视频时存在叙事漂移、角色身份不稳定、跨场景连续性弱以及音视频不匹配等问题。

Method: DramaAgent框架包含：1)故事规划；2)持久角色条件化；3)场景级合成；4)反思引导的针对性修复。框架维护可重用的故事和角色状态，诊断身份漂移、语义缺失、时间不连续和跨模态不匹配等故障，并以特定阶段方式修复问题片段。

Result: 在多个视频生成主干网上的实验表明，DramaAgent在长程一致性、角色一致性、叙事保真度和场景级音视频一致性方面优于直接生成和强基线方法。

Conclusion: 分层代理控制是可控制的长时间视听生成的实际可行方向。

Abstract: Recent diffusion and autoregressive models have substantially improved text-to-video generation, yet producing coherent long-form story videos with consistent characters and aligned audio remains challenging. Existing methods often suffer from narrative drift, unstable character identity, weak cross-scene continuity, and audio-visual mismatch over extended sequences. We propose DramaAgent, a hierarchical, agentic, and model-agnostic framework for long-form text-to-video-and-audio generation. Rather than improving the underlying video backbone itself, DramaAgent introduces an upper-level control layer that decomposes generation into story planning, persistent character conditioning, scene-wise synthesis, and reflection-guided targeted repair. The framework maintains reusable story and character states across scenes, diagnoses failures such as identity drift, missing scene semantics, temporal discontinuity, and cross-modal mismatch, and repairs problematic clips in a stage-specific manner. Experiments across multiple video generation backbones show that DramaAgent improves long-horizon coherence, character consistency, narrative fidelity, and scene-level audio-visual consistency over direct generation and strong baselines. These results suggest that hierarchical agentic control is a practical direction for controllable long-form audiovisual generation. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/DramaAgent. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/DramaAgent.

</details>


### [13] [A Low Grounding Score Is Not an Ungrounded Judge: Identifying the Perceptibility Confound in Multimodal Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00111)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉 grounding探测方法（counterfactual probe）在评估多模态模型判断器是否真正使用图像证据时的有效性问题。研究发现现有的Verdict Grounding Score存在系统性缺陷：只能检测到判断器未使用图像的情况，但无法检测到判断器实际使用了图像的情况，导致假阳性（false alarm）。作者提出了识别和量化这种缺失信息的方法，并在9个判断器上验证了预测的排序，发现典型判断器仅对约一半可解析的属性编辑做出响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型判断器（model judges）在大规模多模态系统中的应用，需要验证这些判断器是否真正使用了视觉证据。现有通过counterfactual probe评估视觉grounding的方法存在系统性偏差，需要深入分析和修正。

Method: 提出Verdict Grounding Score方法：通过编辑图像使ground truth翻转，保持推理轨迹不变，检查判决是否随之改变。同时形式化定义了评分规则，并证明该评分存在单向误差。研究了在假设条件下如何从审计协议收集的三个量来识别缺失信息。

Result: 发现Verdict Grounding Score存在系统性局限：只能使判断器看起来grounding不足，但无法使其看起来过度grounding。对9个判断器的审计表明，预测的排序严格成立，典型判断器仅对约一半可解析的属性编辑做出响应。应用保守拒绝阈值可确认若干单元为假阳性，其中最清晰的是一个能检测到注入错误但在评分上表现得像未使用图像的判断器。

Conclusion: 图像侧counterfactual score不应单独报告。检测probe应在未编辑图像上运行以建立上界，并确认假阳性，这只需极小额外成本。

Abstract: Model judges now supervise multimodal systems at scale, filtering training data, selecting outputs, and supplying the reward that shapes multimodal reasoning models. Trusting one means first checking that it uses its evidence, and that check is itself worth scrutinizing, so we ask whether a counterfactual probe of visual grounding measures what it claims to. The probe edits the image so the ground truth flips, holds the reasoning trace fixed, and asks whether the verdict follows. We formalize it as the Verdict Grounding Score and show it cannot be read the way such scores are read. A verdict responds only to an edit that reaches the judge's decision-relevant reading, so the score is capped by how perceptible the edit is, and unless editing makes the attribute easier to read, the error is one-sided: the score can only make a judge look less grounded than it is. The practical failure is therefore a false alarm, an auditor discarding a usable overseer. Under assumptions we state, we show this missing quantity is not merely bounded but identified from three quantities the same audit protocol already collects, which makes the false-alarm rate directly measurable rather than merely a concern. Auditing nine judges, we find the predicted ordering holds strictly across our entire primary pool, and the typical judge there acts on only about half of the edits whose attribute it can otherwise resolve. Applying a conservative rejection threshold certifies several cells as false alarms outright, the clearest being a judge that detects the injected error essentially every time while still scoring as if it had not used the image at all. The rule that follows is that an image-side counterfactual score should never be reported alone: a detection probe on the unedited image upper-bounds it, certifies its false alarms, and costs nothing extra to run.

</details>


### [14] [Evaluating the Robustness of Anti-UAV Detection under Controlled Fog Degradation: Fog-Aware Training and Clear-Sky Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00141)
*Gur Levy Birkental,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个针对雾 severity 控制的反无人机检测基准，发现雾对检测性能的影响是非线性的，在轻度至中度雾（beta约0.05-0.10）时性能急剧下降，清晰到最浓雾的mAP@0.5:0.95下降96%。雾感知训练可提升各严重程度下的检测能力，但无法完全防止极端雾下的性能崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉反无人机系统基准仅使用晴空 footage，之前关于恶劣天气的鲁棒性研究将天气条件简单化为有/无两类，导致雾 severity 对无人机检测的影响尚未被深入理解，而实际应用中可靠性在狭窄的能见度范围内就会丧失。

Method: 在 Anti-UAV300 数据集的 RGB 模态上合成10个 severity 级别的雾，比较清晰训练的 YOLOv5m 基线与在清晰和雾图像上训练的雾感知模型。雾使用标准合成方法（基于 beta 值的消光系数）进行控制。

Result: 性能下降剧烈且非线性：轻度到中度雾（beta约0.05-0.10）时性能大幅下降，从清晰到最浓雾的 mAP@0.5:0.95 下降96%，主要由召回率和置信度下降导致。在 mAP@0.5 指标上，该崩溃超过先前基准中最严重的雨滴降解。雾感知训练在所有 severity 下提升检测（最高 +0.320 mAP@0.5:0.95），仅牺牲9.1%的清晰天空精度。

Conclusion: 雾感知训练提高了各 severity 下的检测阈值，但不能防止极端雾下的性能崩溃。可靠性在狭窄的能见度范围内丧失，简单的清晰 vs 恶劣天气测试低估了操作风险，需要更精细的 severity 控制评估方法。

Abstract: Vision-based anti-UAV systems must function in poor visibility, yet most benchmarks use only clear-sky footage, and previous robustness studies treat adverse weather as a simple present/absent condition. As a result, the impact of fog severity on ground-to-air UAV detection remains poorly understood. This work presents the first severity-controlled fog benchmark for this task: synthetic fog at ten severity levels is applied to the RGB modality of the Anti-UAV300 dataset, comparing a clear-trained YOLOv5m baseline to a fog-aware model trained on both clear and foggy images. Detection performance drops sharply and non-linearly: degradation is front-loaded across light-to-moderate fog (beta approximately 0.05-0.10), with a 96% reduction in mAP@0.5:0.95 from clear to thickest fog, mainly due to lost recall and confidence. On the comparable metric (mAP@0.5), this collapse exceeds the most extreme rain degradation reported in the closest prior benchmark. Fog-aware training boosts detection across all severities (up to +0.320 mAP@0.5:0.95) with only a 9.1% drop in clear-sky accuracy, raising the threshold for reliable detection while not preventing collapse under extreme fog. Since reliability is lost within a narrow visibility range, simple clear vs adverse tests underestimate operational risk.

</details>


### [15] [GPEC: Efficient Pre-LLM Gaussian Process Embedding Correction for Cardiac Video Caption Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00196)
*Arefeh Rezaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GPEC方法，通过稀疏变分高斯过程在视觉投影层和语言模型之间学习残差校正，将投影的视觉表示向注释引导的目标移动，以改善心脏超声视频的字幕生成质量，且几乎不增加计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在视频理解和字幕生成方面展现出强大潜力，但在心脏超声等专业医学影像领域的性能可能下降，需要一种模块化且计算高效的方法来改进视觉表示。

Method: GPEC是一种插入在视觉投影层和语言模型之间的预LLM误差校正方法，通过稀疏变分高斯过程（具有诱导点、自然参数变分更新和分块线性核）学习残差校正，将投影的视觉表示向由结构化视频注释构建的目标移动，同时保持原始VideoChat2组件冻结。

Result: 实验表明，GPEC在保持原VideoChat2相同输入和参考条件下，提高了字幕相似度和内容对齐性能，且推理时间开销增加少于0.05秒/视频。

Conclusion: GPEC能够在不需要对预训练多模态骨干进行端到端微调的情况下，以最小计算成本改善专业医学视频领域的字幕生成质量。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for video understanding and caption generation, but their performance may decline in specialized medical imaging domains such as echocardiography. This work introduces Gaussian Process Embedding Correction (GPEC), a modular and computationally efficient pre-LLM error-correction method that improves the visual representations used by VideoChat2 for cardiac ultrasound caption generation. GPEC is inserted between the visual projection layer and the language model and learns a residual correction that moves the projected visual representation toward an annotation-guided target. The target is constructed by converting structured video annotations into qualitative attributes, generating a fixed-format reference caption, and mapping it into the language-model embedding space. The correction is modeled using a sparse variational Gaussian Process with inducing points, natural-parameter variational updates, and a block-wise linear kernel, while the original VideoChat2 components remain frozen.The method is evaluated using representation-level, caption-level, content-oriented, and execution-time metrics by comparing the original VideoChat2 with VideoChat2 + GPEC under identical input and reference conditions. Results show improved caption similarity and content alignment after applying the proposed correction. Furthermore, GPEC adds less than 0.05 s of inference-time overhead per video in the evaluated setting. These findings indicate that GPEC can improve caption generation in specialized medical video domains with minimal computational cost, without requiring end-to-end fine-tuning of the pretrained multimodal backbone.

</details>


### [16] [Query Independent Variable Rate Visual Token Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00204)
*Hongbo Zhang,Zihao Yang,Liuyang Song,Daqian Yang,Haoyang Yao,Yan Wen,Zhengtao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于变换编码的视觉token压缩方法，对每个token分配可变比特率而非简单选择，从而实现一次压缩多查询使用的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法按注意力对token排序来决策保留哪些，但注意力依赖于具体问题。当压缩表示被缓存多轮对话或跨设备传输时，单一问题调优的方法失效，需要问题无关的压缩方案。

Method: 采用变换编码框架，保留所有token但为每个token分配不同比特数。通过测量每个token的率-失真曲线，在固定总比特预算下进行整数规划优化分配比特。不使用任何文本信息，实现问题无关的压缩表示。

Result: 在两个数据集和两种容量下，相等比特预算时比均匀编码、水填法和失真排序剪枝更好地保持模型输出分布和答案。与注意力排序剪枝在其调优问题上表现相当，但在同一图像不同问题上表现更优。

Conclusion: 变换编码配合可变token比特率的方法能产生问题无关的压缩表示，在多查询场景下显著优于传统选择式压缩方法。

Abstract: Visual-token compression for vision--language models is posed almost entirely as a selection problem: decide which tokens to keep and discard the rest. The criteria that work best rank tokens by the attention the language model pays them, which makes the ranking a function of the question being asked. That is invisible in a single-turn benchmark and decisive whenever a compressed representation is written once and read many times, as when it is cached across the turns of a conversation or transmitted between a device and a server. We take the other half of the classical transform-coding toolkit instead: keep every token and vary its rate. A transform code exposes each token's measured distortion--rate curve, and a fixed bit budget is distributed across tokens by exact integer rate--distortion optimisation on those curves. No text enters the pipeline, so one compressed representation serves any query. At equal bit budgets, on two datasets and two capacities, it preserves the model's output distribution and its answers better than uniform-rate coding, the closed-form water-fill and distortion-ranked pruning. It matches attention-ranked pruning on the question pruning was tuned for, and overtakes it once the compressed image must answer a different question about the same image.

</details>


### [17] [Multi-Resolution Feature Fusion U-Net for Magnetic Resonance Imaging Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00279)
*Eirini Cholopoulou,Dimitrios E. Diamantis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为MRFFU-Net的新型深度学习架构，用于MRI图像分割。该架构通过多分辨率特征融合模块(MRFF)整合注意力机制和跳跃连接，以捕捉细粒度细节和全局上下文信息，在脑脊液和左心房分割任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习架构(如U-Net)在医学图像分割任务中表现出色，但在保留细粒度细节和全局上下文信息方面存在不足。MRI数据分割尤其具有挑战性，因为解剖结构具有不规则边界、形状、对比度和尺度的变化。

Method: 提出多分辨率特征融合模块(MRFF)，可集成到任何U-Net类架构中。MRFF集成在编码器-解码器的所有层级中，结合注意力机制和跳跃连接，在多个分辨率下提取特征，使模型能够同时捕捉细粒度细节和全局上下文信息。

Result: 在两个公开的MRI基准数据集上进行评估：脊髓MR扫描中的脑脊液(CSF)分割和医学分割十项赛(MSD)挑战中的左心房心脏分割。实验结果表明，MRFFU-Net在多个评估指标上优于最先进模型。

Conclusion: MRFFU-Net在MRI分割任务中表现出色，证明了多分辨率特征融合模块和注意力机制结合的有效性，为不同解剖结构的MRI分割提供了一种有效的深度学习解决方案。

Abstract: The segmentation of anatomical structures in medical images and particularly in MRI scans, is essential for clinical diagnosis and monitoring disease progression. While Deep Learning (DL) architectures, such as U-Net and its extensions are very effective in medical image segmentation tasks, they often struggle with preserving fine-grained details and global contextual information. This is especially challenging for MRI data segmentation, where anatomical structures are characterized by irregular boundaries and variations in shape, contrast, and scale. To address this challenge, we propose a novel DL architecture for MRI segmentation across different anatomical structures. Specifically, the architecture introduces a module, named Multi-Resolution Feature Fusion (MRFF), that can be easily integrated into any U-Net-like architecture. The MRFF is integrated in all levels of an encode-decoder structure, along with attention mechanisms and skip connections to extract features at multiple resolutions, enabling the model to capture both fine-grained details and global contextual information. We evaluate the MRFFU-Net on two publicly available benchmark MRI datasets of different anatomical targets; one for Cerebrospinal Fluid (CSF) segmentation in spinal MR scans, and one for left atrium cardiac segmentation, from the Medical Segmentation Decathlon (MSD) challenge. Experimental results indicate that MRFFU-Net outperforms state-of-the-art models across multiple evaluation metrics, demonstrating its effectiveness in MRI segmentation.

</details>


### [18] [LENS-GRF: Permutation-Invariant Lesion Evidence Network with Gated Residual Fusion for Acne Severity Grading and Multi-Rater Clinical Oracle Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00294)
*Muhammad Muhtasim Shahriar,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LENS-GRF是一种用于四分类痤疮严重程度分级的可解释多阶段框架，结合全局上下文和局部病变证据，在标准数据集上性能优异，但跨域泛化面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 痤疮严重程度分级需要同时兼顾全脸上下文信息和细粒度病变证据，现有方法难以同时有效利用这两种信息。

Method: 提出LENS-GRF框架，集成：1) 自适应面部皮肤分割；2) 全局Vision Transformer先验；3) 置换不变Lesion Set Transformer编码局部病变patch和空间几何；4) 门控残差融合自适应控制局部贡献。使用YOLOv11s进行病变检测。

Result: ACNE04自动分级准确率80.82%，使用GT标注达95.89%±0.59%，QWK 0.9753；跨域零样本评估中自动化LENS-GRF准确率35%，低于全局基线42.50%；Oracle分析显示标注者间一致性49.32%-66.22%，体现检测器域偏移和标注变异性。

Conclusion: LENS-GRF在ACNE04数据集上取得了优异性能（自动80.82%，使用GT标注95.89%），但在跨域零样本评估中性能显著下降（35%），表明存在检测器域偏移和标注变异性问题。数据完整性审计发现15对跨分割重复图像（含5对标签冲突），强调了在医学影像数据集构建中质量控制的重要性。

Abstract: Automated acne severity grading requires both whole-face context and fine-grained lesion evidence. We propose LENS-GRF (Lesion Evidence Network with Set-Transformer and Gated Residual Fusion), an interpretable multi-stage framework for four-class acne severity grading. The method combines Adaptive Facial Skin Segmentation and a global Vision Transformer prior with a permutation-invariant Lesion Set Transformer that encodes localized lesion patches and spatial geometry. Gated Residual Fusion adaptively controls the local residual contribution and reduces to the global prediction when the gate is zero. On ACNE04, fully automated LENS-GRF with YOLOv11s achieved 80.82% accuracy; with ground-truth lesion annotations, it achieved 95.89% +/- 0.59% accuracy and a Quadratic Weighted Kappa of 0.9753. A data-integrity audit identified 15 cross-split duplicate image pairs, including five with conflicting severity labels. In locked zero-shot evaluation on the full PLSBRACNE01 cohort (200 subjects, 600 views), automated LENS-GRF achieved 35.00% accuracy versus 42.50% for the global baseline. On the 148-subject common cohort used for three-dermatologist oracle analysis, ground-truth lesion inputs increased the best oracle accuracy to 47.97%, while the highest oracle QWK was 0.5799. Pairwise oracle agreement ranged from 49.32% to 66.22%, highlighting detector domain shift, annotation variability, and cross-criterion mismatch.

</details>


### [19] [Decoding the Disaster: Multi-Task Geospatial Reasoning with Vision-Language Models and Crowdsourced Imagery for Disaster Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00302)
*Wenping Yin,Fabian Desuer,Ziqi Liu,Naixia Mou,Weijia Li,Pedram Ghamisi,Xiao Xiang Zhu,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRDisaster框架利用视觉语言模型(VLM)对众包灾难图像进行地理定位和空间推理，实现可解释的灾难损害评估。该框架整合了确定性与概率性跨视图地理定位、多视图融合，以及两套空间推理指标用于跨视图验证和灾难严重程度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包图像虽能提供及时、细粒度的街景观察以辅助灾害制图，但通常结构松散、空间模糊且缺乏可靠的地理元数据，导致人工地理定位和解释劳动密集且难以扩展。

Method: 1)构建基于PhotoMappers的基准数据集，包含26,340张图像，涵盖2018-2024年多个灾难事件的VGI街景与RSI跨视图三元组；2)结合确定性与概率性跨视图地理定位与多视图融合，将VGI图像与地理参考的街景和遥感图像关联；3)引入两套空间推理指标，分别用于跨视图地理定位验证和灾难损害评估，利用结构、环境和全局场景线索结合专家验证注释评估灾难严重程度。

Result: 提供了首个系统性的VLM空间推理研究及统一评估框架，通过跨视图地理定位验证、可解释空间推理和感知损害的严重程度评估，将众包灾难图像转化为可操作的地理人工智能(GeoAI)。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统性地探索了基于VLM的空间推理如何通过跨视图地理定位验证、解释性空间推理和损害感知严重程度评估，将众包灾难图像转化为可操作的GeoAI，为灾难应急响应提供新思路。

Abstract: Crowdsourced imagery provides timely, fine-grained, street-level observations for disaster mapping, complementing conventional remote sensing imagery (RSI) during emergency response. However, such imagery is often unstructured, spatially ambiguous, and lacks reliable geographic metadata, making manual geolocalization and interpretation labor-intensive and difficult to scale. This work proposes a multi-task Geospatial Reasoning Disaster mapping framework, namely GRDisaster, to examine the potential of vision-language models (VLMs) in understanding, geolocalizing, and reasoning over crowdsourced disaster imagery. GRDisaster is built on a newly curated benchmark dataset derived from PhotoMappers, comprising 26,340 images organized into human-validated volunteered geographic information (VGI), street-view imagery (SVI), RSI cross-view triplets covering multiple disaster events from 2018 to 2024. The framework combines deterministic and probabilistic cross-view geolocalization with multi-view fusion to associate VGI images with georeferenced SVI and RSI. It introduces two sets of spatial reasoning indicators for cross-view geolocalization validation and disaster damage assessment. These indicators use structural, environmental, and global-scene cues to validate cross-view correspondences and visually observable damage evidence with expert-verified annotations to assess disaster severity, improving the interpretability of VLM outputs. To our knowledge, this study provides the first systematic investigation and unified evaluation framework for examining how VLM-based spatial reasoning can transform crowdsourced disaster imagery into actionable geospatial artificial intelligence (GeoAI) through cross-view geolocalization validation, interpretable spatial reasoning, and damage-aware severity assessment.

</details>


### [20] [Beyond Pixel Reconstruction: Retrieval-Guided Glyph-Aware Restoration for Low-Resource Manchu Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00315)
*Ting Huang,Dongdong Wang,Mingqiu Liang,Siyang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于检索的 glyph感知修复框架，用于恢复资源匮乏的历史满文文献。该方法通过检索相关 glyph示例来提供结构指导，在低资源条件下改善了退化字符结构的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档修复方法主要优化像素级重建，无法保留满文字符的结构特性，且历史满文文献数字化受严重退化和小样本训练数据的限制。

Method: 提出检索引导的 glyph感知修复框架，检索相关 glyph示例提供结构指导，并将这些信息集成到重建过程中。

Result: 在满文历史文档上的广泛实验表明，该方法在图像修复质量和 glyph级保真度方面均优于现有修复方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对于低资源历史文档的可靠修复，融入字符感知的结构先验具有重要意义。

Abstract: Historical Manchu documents preserve invaluable linguistic and cultural heritage, yet their digitization is hindered by severe degradations and the scarcity of paired training data. Existing document restoration methods primarily optimize pixel-level reconstruction, which can produce visually plausible results while failing to preserve the structural identity of Manchu glyphs. To address this limitation, we propose a retrieval-guided glyph-aware restoration framework that goes beyond pixel reconstruction by explicitly incorporating glyph-level structural knowledge. Our method retrieves relevant glyph exemplars to provide structural guidance during restoration and integrates this information into the reconstruction process, improving the recovery of degraded character structures under low-resource conditions. Extensive experiments on Manchu historical documents demonstrate that the proposed approach improves both image restoration quality and glyph-level fidelity compared with existing restoration methods. These results highlight the importance of incorporating character-aware structural priors for reliable restoration of low-resource historical documents.

</details>


### [21] [EgoRefine: Ego-Referenced Predictive Alignment and Trajectory-Conditioned Reliability-Aware Fusion for Asynchronous Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00319)
*Lingzhao Kong,Yongsheng Zang,Yu Kang,Kailun Yang,Jie Fu,Yukun Zuo,Zhiyong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoRefine是一个用于异步协作感知的三维目标检测框架，通过自我参照的预测对齐和可靠性感知融合来补偿协作特征的时间延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在异步通信环境下补偿协作特征时主要依赖发送代理自身的历史信息，导致与自我代理当前观测存在残余对齐误差，且后续融合通常忽略对齐质量的空间变化。

Method: 提出EgoRefine框架，包含两个模块：1）自我参照预测对齐模块，使用当前自我特征引导协作轨迹场预测，并沿自我参照轨迹方向细化采样偏移；2）轨迹条件可靠性感知融合模块，将自我与协作流之间的轨迹差异和对齐细化幅度作为对齐线索，自适应重新加权两个流后再进行卷积融合。

Result: 在V2V4Real和DAIR-V2X-Seq数据集上，EgoRefine相比TraF-Align在AP@0.5和AP@0.7上分别提升1.6和2.9个百分点。

Conclusion: EgoRefine通过引入自我参照机制和可靠性感知融合，有效解决了异步协作感知中的特征对齐问题，显著提升了三维目标检测性能。

Abstract: Collaborative perception enables connected agents to share complementary observations for 3D object detection, extending sensing range and mitigating occlusion. Under asynchronous communication, however, cooperative features arrive with temporal delay. Existing prediction-based methods compensate for these features mainly from the transmitting agent's own history, leaving residual misalignment with the ego agent's current observation; subsequent fusion also often overlooks spatial variations in alignment quality. We propose EgoRefine, an ego-referenced predictive alignment and reliability-aware fusion framework for asynchronous collaborative perception. Its Ego-referenced Predictive Alignment module uses the current ego feature to guide cooperative trajectory-field prediction and refines the sampling offsets along an ego-referenced trajectory direction. Its Trajectory-conditioned Reliability-aware Fusion module treats the trajectory discrepancy between the ego and cooperative streams and the directional refinement magnitude as alignment cues, using them to condition the relation between aligned features and adaptively reweight the two streams before convolutional fusion. Experiments on V2V4Real and DAIR-V2X-Seq show that EgoRefine outperforms TraF-Align by 1.6 and 2.9 points on average in AP@0.5 and AP@0.7, respectively. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/godk0509/EgoRefine.

</details>


### [22] [LEGO-OPD: Factorized Teacher Composition for Multimodal On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00333)
*Jaeyun Shin,Hangeol Chang,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LEGO-OPD是一种多模态策略蒸馏方法，通过将语言专家和接地专家解耦为先验和似然分别监督，实现视觉接地与语言推理的独立控制，并用自适应校准动态调整视觉监督强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接使用VLM的完整预测分布会将视觉接地信号与语言先验纠缠在一起，阻碍接地信息的独立传递；增强视觉监督虽能改善感知但可能过度强调视觉证据而损害语言推理能力。

Method: 提出因子化组合方法：将语言专家作为候选token的先验分布，接地专家提供视觉似然来更新先验而非直接转移完整预测分布；引入自适应校准机制，以接地专家的图像诱导预测偏移作为前缀相关参考，动态决定视觉似然更新语言先验的强度。

Result: 在Qwen3模型上的实验表明，LEGO-OPD在多模态和纯文本推理任务上均一致优于评估的单教师和多教师OPD基线方法，同时提升学生模型的视觉感知能力并保持纯文本推理能力。

Conclusion: 因子化组合和自适应校准能够有效解耦视觉接地与语言推理的监督，实现两者的独立控制，为多模态策略蒸馏提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Multimodal on-policy distillation (OPD) aims to improve visual grounding while preserving the strong reasoning capabilities of language models. Recent multi-teacher approaches combine LLM and VLM teachers to provide complementary supervision. However, directly using a VLM's full predictive distribution entangles its visual grounding signal with its own language prior, preventing the grounding information from being transferred independently. Conversely, increasing the strength of visual supervision can improve perception but may overemphasize visual evidence and degrade language reasoning. To address this trade-off, we introduce LEGO-OPD, which selectively composes factors from a Language Expert and a Grounding expert into One teacher distribution for multimodal OPD. Under a generalized Bayesian formulation, the language expert provides a prior over candidate tokens, while the grounding expert contributes a visual likelihood that updates this prior, rather than transferring its complete predictive distribution. This factorized composition allows language reasoning and visual grounding to be controlled independently. We further introduce adaptive calibration to determine how strongly the visual likelihood should update the language prior at each decoding prefix. Specifically, LEGO-OPD uses the grounding expert's image-induced prediction shift as a prefix-dependent reference, preventing both insufficient and excessive visual supervision. Experiments with Qwen3 models show that LEGO-OPD consistently outperforms the evaluated single- and multi-teacher OPD baselines on both multimodal and text-only reasoning tasks. Moreover, it improves the initial student's visual perception while preserving text-only reasoning.

</details>


### [23] [Vmem-$\varphi$: Low-Compute Out-of-Distribution Detection in Spiking Neural Networks from Membrane-Potential Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00350)
*Arul Rana,Agrim Tripathi,Shoaib Ahmed Dipu,Md. Shaown Miah,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed,Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用SNNs的子阈值膜电位(V_mem(t))作为内部信号进行分布外检测(OOD)，并构建了Gen1-C事件相机腐败基准数据集和MDD方法，在64帧观察窗口下实现高效低成本的OOD检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法依赖目标检测SNN中不可用的模型输出或计算组件，且不适合低算力部署场景，因此需要一种适用于SNN的轻量级OOD检测方法。

Method: 提出利用子阈值膜电位的简单per-channel统计数据进行OOD检测，并设计了一种腐败盲(blind)的Multi-Descriptor Deviation (MDD)方法，在64帧观察窗口内运行。

Result: 在Gen1-C基准的最高腐败 severity下，MDD在6种腐败中的5种达到AUROC超过0.88，剩余一种腐败也是对检测器影响最小的。

Conclusion: 时间膜电位动态可提供一种有效且低成本的SNN事件感知OOD检测信号。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer an energy-efficient approach to processing event-camera data, yet out-of-distribution (OOD) detection remains challenging in this setting. Existing OOD detection methods often depend on model outputs or computational components that are unavailable in object detection SNNs or are poorly suited to low-compute deployment. To that effect, we show that the subthreshold membrane potential \(V_{{\mathrm{{mem}}}}(t)\) provides a useful internal signal for detecting distribution shifts. Simple per-channel statistics derived from these membrane dynamics enable OOD detection. To evaluate this approach, we introduce Gen1-C, an event-camera corruption benchmark developed upon the Prophesee Gen1 automotive detection dataset, containing six sensor-motivated histogram-level stress tests at five severity levels. We further propose the Multi-Descriptor Deviation (MDD), a corruption-blind method that operates on membrane-potential statistics. At the highest corruption severity, MDD achieves an AUROC of more than 0.88 on five of the six corruptions using only a bounded 64-frame observation window. Notably, the remaining corruption is also the one that has the smallest effect on the underlying detector. These results show that the temporal membrane-potential dynamics can provide an effective and low-cost signal for OOD detection in SNN-based event perception.

</details>


### [24] [From Image Latent Space to Fuzzy Rules: Interpretable Analysis of Gastrointestinal Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00414)
*Michael D. Vasilakakis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于原型和模糊规则的解释框架，用于解释预训练基础模型在医学影像任务中的特征表示，无需微调即可实现可解释的分类，并能生成人类可读的IF-THEN规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在医学影像任务中表现出强大的迁移能力，但其潜在表示如何编码临床相关信息仍不明确，在安全关键领域缺乏可解释性是一个重要挑战。

Method: 通过在特征空间中进行聚类学习类别特定原型，将patch特征表达为原型相似度，使用模糊规则（IF-THEN条件）进行分类。在ViT-S/16 backbone（ImageNet-1K和GastroNet-5M预训练）的最后两个模块上应用。

Result: 无需 backbone 微调，准确率与k-NN、kernel SVM、线性探测等黑盒分类器相当。领域特定预训练产生的特征既具有判别性又具有符号可压缩性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了透明、深度可解析的基础模型视角，可用于调查合成医学图像，局部化合成图像与真实组织的差异，为安全关键医学应用提供可解释性保障。

Abstract: Foundation models pretrained on large-scale datasets demonstrate strong transferability to medical imaging tasks. However, understanding how their latent representations encode clinically relevant information remains an open challenge in safety-critical domains. This study proposes a prototype-based fuzzy-rule framework that interprets the patch-level features produced by the inner layers of pretrained foundation models, without any fine-tuning. Class-specific prototypes are learned by clustering in the feature space, yielding compact visual patterns. Patch features are then expressed as prototype similarities and classified by fuzzy rules with linguistic IF-THEN conditions that are human readable. The framework is applied across the final two blocks of ViT-S/16 backbones pretrained on ImageNet-1K and GastroNet-5M, and benchmarked against k-nearest neighbours, kernel SVM, and linear probing under identical frozen features, on wireless capsule endoscopy classification, gastrointestinal endoscopy classification, and colonic polyp segmentation. The experimental analysis shows that the proposed method, without backbone fine-tuning, reaches accuracy comparable to these black-box classifiers, and that domain-specific pretraining yields features that are both discriminative and symbolically compressible. Because the resulting rules are extracted from real data and expressed in interpretable terms, they are further used as an instrument to investigate synthetic medical images, providing a human-readable account of which real prototypes and rules a generator reproduces or fails to reproduce, localising where a synthetic image departs from real tissue rather than summarising it with a single score. The framework thus offers a transparent, depth-resolved view of how foundation models organise clinically relevant structure, together with a practical downstream use of the extracted rules.

</details>


### [25] [Scores That Hold, Benchmarks That Leak: Measuring Dataset Contamination in Public Brain-Tumor MRI Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00421)
*Bhanu Prakash Vangala,Sowmya Guda,Latha Peddi,Navya Vangala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了脑肿瘤MRI分类深度学习基准数据集存在严重的数据泄露问题，即使移除所有识别的泄露测试图像后模型性能仍基本不变，说明现有基准的准确率指标无法真正反映模型的肿瘤识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准数据集的准确率虽高达98%以上，但准确率无法衡量数据集质量的一个关键维度——数据完整性，即测试集与训练集在图像、患者和采集来源层面是否独立。

Method: 提出三层污染框架（重复、患者和来源标签泄露），对三个最广泛使用的脑肿瘤数据集进行审计，使用胸片阴性对照，并量化九种架构和三种评估条件下各层泄露对性能的影响。

Result: 各层污染严重：主流数据集28.8%的官方测试集图像在训练集中存在近孪生兄弟；第二个数据集22.3%的测试图像字节完全相同；95.5%的可追溯测试图像与训练集共享患者；不含解剖信息的文件头特征以0.959的平衡准确率区分肿瘤与非肿瘤。但移除所有识别的泄露测试图像后，平衡准确率基本不变。

Conclusion: 数据集完整性是一个独立的、可测量的基准质量维度，稳定的前排排名无法证明基准完整性；仅凭这些数据集上的准确率无法证明模型学会了识别肿瘤而非利用数据集特定的线索。

Abstract: Automated classification of brain tumors from MRI is a heavily published application of deep learning in medical imaging, with reported accuracies on public benchmarks routinely exceeding 98%. However, accuracy does not capture a critical dimension of benchmark quality: dataset integrity, defined as the independence of test from training data at the image, patient, and acquisition-source levels. We introduce a three-layer contamination framework comprising duplicate, patient, and source-label leakage to assess the public corpora on which this literature rests. We audit the three most widely used corpora against a chest-radiograph negative control and quantify each layer's effect on measured performance across nine architectures and three evaluation conditions. Contamination is severe at every layer: 28.8% of the dominant corpus's official test split has a near-twin in its own training split, a second corpus leaks 22.3% of its test images byte-identically, 95.5% of traceable test images share a patient with training, and file-header features containing no anatomy separate tumor from no-tumor at 0.959 balanced accuracy, at parity with fine-tuned ResNet backbones. The unexpected result is that removing every identified leaked test image leaves balanced accuracy essentially unchanged: stable performance after deduplication does not establish benchmark integrity. Our findings establish dataset integrity as a distinct, measurable axis of benchmark quality that a stable leaderboard cannot certify. For biomedical research, reported accuracy on these corpora alone does not establish that a model has learned to recognize tumors rather than exploit dataset-specific cues. We release the contaminated-file lists, recovered patient identifiers, and deduplicated splits.

</details>


### [26] [PACT: End-to-End Learning of Human Pose, Contacts, and Forces from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00451)
*Rikhat Akizhanov,Yangsong Zhang,Nikolai Kaliazin,Peter Wolf,Yoshihiko Nakamura,Pascal Fua,Fabio Pizzati,Ivan Laptev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACT是一个端到端模型,能从单目视频中联合估计人体姿态、接触点和接触力。该方法通过在人体重建基础模型上添加可学习的接触力标记和时间transformer,结合物理监督来处理力标注稀缺的问题,并构建了ForceWall爬墙基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将视觉姿态重建与接触力和估计分离,这种分离限制了联合推理并可能在阶段间传播错误。需要一种端到端的联合学习方法来同时估计人体姿态、接触点和接触力。

Method: 1. 扩展人体重建基础模型,添加可学习的接触力token;2. 使用时间transformer整合视觉特征与世界空间运动;3. 联合预测头细化人体姿态并估计接触点和力;4. 物理基础监督确保重建运动与交互力的一致性;5. 开发数据标注管道,结合接触标注与基于物理的运动和力优化,为合成和真实视频生成训练监督。

Result: 在接触力和估计方面达到最先进水平,优于分阶段重建方法,并能泛化到训练分布之外的交互任务。同时发布了ForceWall爬墙基准数据集,包含爬墙视频和来自力传感器的真实接触力真值。

Conclusion: 端到端联合学习是從视频中恢复人体运动和物理交互的有效方法。该方法在接触检测和力估计任务上展现优异性能,并具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Human motion, environmental contacts, and interaction forces are governed by common physical laws, yet existing approaches typically separate visual pose reconstruction from contact and force estimation. This separation limits joint reasoning and can propagate errors between stages. We introduce PACT, an end-to-end model that jointly learns to estimate human pose, contacts and contact forces from monocular video. Our approach augments a human reconstruction foundation model with learnable contact-force tokens and a temporal transformer that integrates visual features with world-space motion. Joint prediction heads refine human poses and estimate contacts and forces, while physics-based supervision encourages consistency between the reconstructed motion and interaction forces. To address the scarcity of force annotations, we develop a data annotation pipeline that combines contact labeling with physics-based motion and force optimization, producing training supervision from synthetic and real-world videos. We also introduce a real-world climbing benchmark ForceWall with climbing videos and corresponding ground-truth contact forces obtained from the force sensors. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art contact and force estimation, outperforming staged reconstruction approaches and generalizing to interactions beyond the training distribution. These results support end-to-end joint learning as an effective approach to recovering human motion and physical interactions from video.

</details>


### [27] [PixelDense: Dense Prediction as Representation Alignment for Pixel Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00483)
*Lehan Yang,Daiqing Qi,Wenhao Zhang,Avery Li,Yiqing Yang,Yifan Li,Yu Kong,Haitian Zheng,Zhifei Zhang,Zhe Lin,Varun Jampani,Sheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixelDense通过将语义教师(DINOv2、SAM2)和几何教师(Depth Anything v2、Metric3D v2)分离到不同的投影流中，并引入权重空间正交性惩罚，解决了多教师REPA中语义和几何梯度竞争的问题，显著提升了图像生成质量和编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REPA虽然能加速扩散变换器训练，但现有方法使用语义编码器作为对齐目标。最新研究表明空间结构而非全局语义才是对齐效果的关键载体，但用于预测该结构的密集预测基础模型作为REPA目标一直被忽视。当简单合并所有教师时，语义和几何梯度竞争同一个去噪器投影，导致性能下降。

Method: 提出PixelDense方法：1)将DINOv2和SAM2通过语义投影流路由；2)将Depth Anything v2和Metric3D v2通过几何投影流路由；3)添加权重空间正交性惩罚使两个流保持在不相交的子空间中。训练时冻结所有教师，推理时丢弃。

Result: 在PixelGen和DeCo上使用单一配方，PixelDense在GenEval、DPG-Bench和HPS v2.1上均有提升。PixelGen-XXL的GenEval Overall从0.7927提升至0.8093，超越所有单一教师和未分解的多教师变体。部分噪声重建中，独立的全景、深度和表面法线探针在COCO和Flickr30K上达到最高53.1%的PQ增益和36.0%的深度AbsRel降低。从随机初始化开始，PixelDense达到基线峰值GenEval的速度快1.23倍。PIE-Bench编辑中，PixelDense在每个编辑强度下保留更多源背景和布局，背景PSNR最高提升2.2 dB。

Conclusion: 空间结构是REPA对齐效果的关键载体；将语义和几何教师分离到不同投影流并保持正交性可有效避免梯度竞争；几何教师(深度估计)优于语义教师(分割)；PixelDense是一种简单有效的多教师REPA训练策略。

Abstract: Representation alignment (REPA) accelerates diffusion transformer training, but its alignment targets are almost exclusively semantic encoders such as DINOv2 and CLIP. Recent analysis points to spatial structure, not global semantics, as the carrier of the alignment effect, yet dense-prediction foundation models trained to predict that structure remain overlooked as REPA targets. In pixel-space diffusion, SAM2, Depth Anything v2, and Metric3D v2 each outperform the DINOv2-only GenEval baseline, with the two geometric teachers leading the segmentation teacher. A flat sum of all four teachers, however, lands below the best single geometric teacher, as semantic and geometric gradients compete for one denoiser projection. We introduce PixelDense, which routes DINOv2 and SAM2 through a semantic projection stream, routes Depth Anything v2 and Metric3D v2 through a geometric projection stream, and adds a weight-space orthogonality penalty that keeps the two streams in disjoint subspaces. All four teachers are frozen during training and dropped at inference. Applied to PixelGen and DeCo with a single recipe, PixelDense improves GenEval, DPG-Bench, and HPS v2.1, raises PixelGen-XXL's GenEval Overall from 0.7927 to 0.8093, and beats every single-teacher and unfactored multi-teacher variant. In partial-noise reconstruction, independent panoptic, depth, and surface-normal probes show up to 53.1% PQ gain and 36.0% depth AbsRel reduction at $τ=0.5$ across COCO and Flickr30K. From random initialization, PixelDense also reaches the baseline's peak GenEval 1.23x faster. In SDEdit editing on PIE-Bench, PixelDense keeps more of the source background and layout at every edit strength, raising background PSNR by up to 2.2 dB.

</details>


### [28] [Memorizon: Training World Models Beyond Their Context Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00544)
*Tingting Liao,Xuezhi Liang,Hao Li,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Memorizon是一种用于流式世界模型的训练方法，通过有界记忆库机制解决长跨度监督的二次复杂度问题，使得在无需处理所有中间帧的情况下实现跨访问的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式世界模型需要在重复访问时保持场景一致性，但密集注意力机制导致长跨度监督成本呈二次增长，限制了模型处理长时序关系的能力。

Method: 提出有界记忆库机制：训练样本覆盖任意长度跨度，但仅在最后k个chunk上评分；每个评分的chunk通过相机共可见性检索top-K潜在变量，形成共享记忆库，库大小由kK限定，与span长度无关。

Result: 相比滑动窗口基线，检索机制在所有数据分割上提升了重访一致性；足够长的跨度（达到每次返回的首次访问）进一步提升24%-30%的一致性，但图像质量有所下降；超过该跨度后更长跨度不再带来收益；从其他episode填充记忆库会使相关性降低83%，证明模型确实使用了检索内容。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解耦了长跨度监督需求与注意力计算成本，使得将跨度从100秒扩展到400秒仅增加12%的步时间，为长时序世界模型训练提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Streaming world models should render a place consistently across repeated visits. Directly supervising such revisits requires training samples that capture both visits, often spanning minutes. Yet dense attention over the full span incurs quadratic costs, making long-span supervision expensive. Memorizon breaks this coupling: long spans are needed for supervision, but not for attention, since the two visits can share a forward pass without including every intervening frame. A training sample covers a span of any length but is scored only on its last $k$ chunks. Instead of tokenizing the history before them, each scored chunk retrieves its own top-$K$ latents by camera co-visibility, and the union of these requests forms a shared bank. The bank is bounded by $kK$, so the sequence stays bounded however long the span; at the shortest span the recipe is exactly conventional training. Adding the bank raises the cost of a step once; beyond that, a longer span costs little, and going from 100 to 400 s adds 12% to the step time. Against a sliding-window baseline, retrieval raises revisit consistency on every split, and a span long enough to reach the first visit of each return adds a further 24% to 30%, at some cost in image quality; beyond that span, more length no longer helps. Filling the bank from another episode lowers revisit correlation by 83%, so the model uses what it retrieves. Project page: https://tingtingliao.github.io/memorizon

</details>


### [29] [PhysVista: Benchmarking Physical Intelligence in VLMs via a Perception-Reasoning-Assessment Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00559)
*Xinge Peng,Yiting Lu,Tianwu Zhi,Wen Wen,Jianzhao Liu,Xin Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysVista是一个用于评估视觉语言模型(VLMs)物理智能的新基准,通过闭环认知框架综合评估物理状态感知、物理动态推理和物理合理性评估,并区分事件级和尺度级推理以实现细粒度分析,同时包含真实世界和AI生成视频以评估跨领域和新兴生成场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准范式存在碎片化问题,专注于孤立的认知阶段而忽视感知、推理和物理判断之间的协同作用,缺乏整体视角来诊断VLMs是否能可靠评估生成模型的物理真实性,尤其在物理一致性捕获方面存在疑问。

Method: 提出PhysVista基准,采用闭环认知框架(类似于人类"观察-推理-评估"过程),联合评估物理状态感知、物理动态推理和物理合理性评估,并区分事件级推理和尺度级推理实现细粒度分析,涵盖真实世界和AI生成视频。

Result: 跨多种VLM的广泛实验揭示了物理推理和合理性评估的重大局限性,表明视觉识别与真正的物理理解之间存在持续差距。

Conclusion: 当前VLMs在物理推理和 plausibility评估方面存在显著不足,需要更基于物理原则的多模态智能设计,该基准为评估VLMs的物理智能提供了全面框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, yet whether they truly capture the physical consistency underlying real-world dynamics remains unclear. Existing benchmark paradigms often suffer from fragmented evaluation, focusing on isolated cognitive stages while overlooking the inherent synergy between perception, reasoning, and physical judgment. The lack of a holistic perspective limits the ability to diagnose whether VLMs can reliably evaluate the physical authenticity of emerging generative models. To address these issues, we introduce PhysVista, a benchmark designed to evaluate physical intelligence in VLMs through a closed cognitive loop framework inspired by the human seeing-reasoning-assessment process. PhysVista restores this loop by jointly evaluating physical state perception, physical dynamics reasoning, and physical plausibility assessment. It further distinguishes event-level reasoning and scale-level reasoning to enable fine-grained analysis of physical understanding. In addition, PhysVista incorporates both real-world and AI-generated videos, allowing evaluation across diverse domains and emerging generative scenarios. Extensive experiments across a diverse set of VLMs reveal substantial limitations in physical reasoning and plausibility assessment, highlighting a persistent gap between visual recognition and genuine physical understanding, and pointing toward more principled designs for physically grounded multimodal intelligence.

</details>


### [30] [FORTE: Adaptive Scoring and Exact Keyframe Selection for Long-Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00573)
*Haifeng Huang,Biyin Xu,Chunsheng Xin,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FORTE是一个无需训练的查询感知关键帧选择框架,用于帮助多模态大语言模型处理长视频。该框架通过自适应相关性评分和全局关键帧优化两个阶段,在有限评分预算下自适应地为有前景的帧分配评估资源,同时探索未被充分代表的时间区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分的关键帧选择方法通常在固定、均匀采样的候选池中进行搜索,导致该候选池之外的有效证据永远无法被选中。在有限的相关性评分预算下,关键挑战在于如何自适应地为有前景的帧分配评估资源,同时继续探索未被充分代表的时间区域。

Method: FORTE框架包含两个阶段:1)自适应相关性评分:从稀疏、均匀分布的观测开始,使用高效的高斯过程相关性预测器估计未评分帧的相关性,利用时间局部性和近似带状核结构将计算复杂度从立方降至线性;选择下一帧进行评分时平衡预测相关性与时间覆盖。2)全局关键帧优化:通过最大化同时考虑测量相关性和时间覆盖的目标函数来选择最终关键帧,推导出精确算法在时间线性复杂度下识别最优子集。

Result: 在四个长视频问答基准测试上的实验表明,FORTE在每种测试的评分预算下都达到了最高的平均准确率。进一步评估显示其在不同的相关性评分器和下游多模态大语言模型中都表现出一致的有效性。

Conclusion: FORTE通过训练-free的方法成功解决了固定候选池的局限性,自适应地探索视频内容并选择最具相关性的关键帧。该方法在多个基准测试和不同模型配置下展现出优异的性能和泛化能力,为长视频问答任务中的高效帧选择提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Query-aware keyframe selection enables multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to process long videos using only a small set of question-relevant frames. Existing score-based methods, however, typically search within a fixed, uniformly sampled candidate pool, preventing evidence outside this pool from ever being selected. Given a limited relevance-scoring budget, the key challenge is to allocate evaluations adaptively to promising frames while continuing to explore underrepresented temporal regions. We introduce FORTE, a training-free framework that addresses this challenge through two stages: adaptive relevance scoring and global keyframe optimization. Starting from sparse, uniformly distributed observations, our efficient Gaussian-process relevance predictor estimates relevance for unscored frames, exploiting temporal locality and the approximately banded kernel structure to reduce the core computation from cubic to linear time in the number of frames for fixed bandwidth. The scoring stage then selects which frames to score next by balancing predicted relevance with temporal coverage, prioritizing promising regions while also exploring less-represented parts of the video. The optimization stage selects the final keyframes by maximizing an objective that jointly captures measured relevance and temporal coverage. We derive an exact algorithm that leverages the logarithmic coverage structure to identify the optimal subset of the scored candidate pool in time linear in the pool size, for a fixed final-frame budget. Experiments on four long-video question-answering benchmarks show that FORTE achieves the highest observed mean accuracy among the compared selectors under every tested scoring budget. Further evaluations demonstrate its consistent effectiveness across different relevance scorers and downstream MLLMs.

</details>


### [31] [Gestalt: Large Multimodal Interplay Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00576)
*Zequn Yang,Yu Miao,Haotian Ni,Ziheng Chen,Chengxiang Huang,Dongzhan Zhou,Kai Chen,Qi Zhang,Ji-Rong Wen,Yake Wei,Di Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gestalt提出了一种基于多模态交互金字塔的新型大模型架构，通过统一离散扩散框架和可学习的交互token，实现从模态特定处理到跨模态对齐再到深度多模态整合的层次化建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型主要关注如何容纳更多模态，却忽略了各模态的独特特征及模态间关系，导致多模态整合不足。受人类多阶段感知特性的启发，提出多模态交互金字塔来系统化组织多模态建模。

Method: 1) 提出多模态交互金字塔结构，将建模分为模态特定处理、跨模态对齐、深度多模态整合三阶段；2) 采用统一离散扩散框架；3) 设计交互分区架构，用可学习交互token促进跨模态交换与整合；4) 配套数据组织和训练策略。

Result: 在图像生成、多模态理解和纯文本评估三个任务上都取得优异性能，显著提升了跨模态整合能力，同时保留了模态特定信息。

Conclusion: Gestalt有效结合了扩散基多模态模型的优势，为统一多模态智能提供了有前景的发展方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Gestalt, a new paradigm of large multimodal model built around multimodal interplay. Despite rapid advances, large multimodal models are reaching a bottleneck: existing approaches focus primarily on accommodating additional modalities while overlooking the distinct characteristics of each modality and the relations among them. Motivated by the multistage property of human multisensory perception, we propose a multimodal interplay pyramid that organizes multimodal modeling as a progression from modality-specific processing, through cross-modal alignment, to deeper multimodal integration. Guided by this pyramid, Gestalt adopts a unified discrete diffusion framework and an interplay-partitioned architecture, with learnable interplay tokens mediating cross-modal exchange and integration. The pyramid also structures its data organization and training strategy. Strong performance across image generation, multimodal understanding, and text-only evaluation shows that Gestalt significantly improves cross-modal integration while preserving modality-specific information, effectively harnessing the strengths of diffusion-based multimodal models and offering a promising path toward unified multimodal intelligence.

</details>


### [32] [Discrete Annotation, Continuous Preference: Rethinking Supervision for Accurate and Generalizable Aesthetic Image Cropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00582)
*Ziqing Zhang,Xiao Liu,Kai Liu,Jianze Li,Weihang Zhang,Linghe Kong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出连续偏好场(CPF)来解决图像美学裁剪中标注数据存在的人为主观性和固定采样网格的刚性问题，并基于此训练CPIC模型和重新校准基准数据集CPICD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有美学图像裁剪数据集存在两个根本性问题：(1)人为主观性导致标注不一致；(2)受限于固定采样网格的刚性离散性。这些缺陷不仅限制了模型的准确性和泛化能力，还严重扭曲了公平评估。

Method: 提出连续偏好场(CPF)将人类裁剪偏好建模为多峰、连续、尖锐的场，通过峰聚类、离格细化、负向整形和场组装四步从离散标注恢复密集偏好景观。基于CPF训练基于VLM的CPIC模型，使用GRPO和CPF奖励进行优化，解决模板崩溃问题。

Result: CPIC达到最先进性能并具有出色的域外泛化能力；CPICD通过纠正主流基准测试中的网格边界伪影，建立了严格可靠的评估基础。

Conclusion: CPF、CPIC和CPICD在广泛实验和用户研究中展现出优越性，为未来裁剪研究提供了更精确的偏好建模方法和高可靠的评估基准。

Abstract: Aesthetic image cropping aims to identify the optimal crop of an image in terms of aesthetics and composition. While supervision based on annotated data is fundamental, the field has been hindered by a long-standing problem: existing datasets suffer from (1) human subjectivity and (2) rigid discreteness confined to fixed sampling grids. These flawed annotations not only limit the accuracy and generalization of trained models but also severely distort fair evaluation. To overcome this, we propose to model human cropping preference as a multi-peaked, continuous, and sharp field over the crop space. We introduce the Continuous Preference Field (CPF), which recovers a dense preference landscape from discrete annotations through (1) peak clustering, (2) off-lattice refinement, (3) negative shaping, and (4) field assembly. Based on this, we train CPIC, a VLM-based cropping model optimized via GRPO with the CPF reward, which overcomes template collapse, achieving state-of-the-art performance and exceptional out-of-domain generalization. Finally, to resolve the long-standing benchmark evaluation crisis, we introduce CPICD, a comprehensive recalibration of existing ground-truth boxes. By leveraging the CPF to correct grid-bound artifacts across mainstream benchmarks, CPICD establishes a rigorous and reliable foundation for future cropping research. Extensive experiments and user studies demonstrate the superiority of our CPF, CPIC, and CPICD. Code, model, and data are available at https://github.com/zzqingz/CPIC.

</details>


### [33] [Just Align $\bm{{x}}$: Aligning Predictions, Not Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00600)
*Yuyao Zhang,Yuwei Hu,Ziyang Mai,Yu-Wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JAx是一种预测监督方法，通过跨噪声等级对齐干净图像预测来解决扩散模型训练中的表示对齐问题。该方法将噪声更大的学生观察与更干净的观察通过马尔可夫退化耦合，使oracle预测保持与JiT目标相同的条件均值，同时提供更低方差的训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示对齐虽能加速扩散训练，但其好处不能可靠地转移到像素空间干净图像预测。现有的辅助特征对齐方法（如JiT）虽能改善语义特征的获取，但会减少图像变化信息的获取，导致辅助目标与去噪任务之间的不匹配。

Method: JAx将噪声更大的学生观察与更干净的观察通过马尔可夫退化耦合，保留原始JiT输入分布。在这种耦合下，来自更清洁状态的oracle预测具有与最优JiT目标相同的条件均值，而其条件目标协方差不大于JiT目标。实践中结合地面真实监督与可靠性门控耦合带，基于预测风险选择附近的教师状态。

Result: 在ImageNet 256x256上，JAx在JiT-B/16、L/16和H/16上均一致改善FID并加速收敛，无需外部编码器或改变架构和采样程序。梯度诊断显示小批量梯度方差降低，消融实验表明增益不能仅由时间重加权解释。

Conclusion: 预测空间监督为像素空间生成模型提供了一种简单且有原则的表示对齐替代方案。

Abstract: Representation alignment has become an effective way to accelerate diffusion training, but its benefits do not transfer reliably to pixel-space clean-image prediction. In JiT, we find that auxiliary feature alignment can improve access to semantic features while reducing access to image variation needed for clean-image prediction, creating a mismatch between the auxiliary objective and the denoising task. This suggests a different principle: auxiliary supervision should improve the prediction target itself rather than impose a separate representation target. We introduce JAx (Just Align x), a prediction-supervision method that aligns clean-image predictions across noise levels. JAx couples a noisier student observation with a cleaner observation through a Markov degradation that preserves the original JiT input distribution. Under this coupling, the oracle prediction from the cleaner state has the same conditional mean as the optimal JiT target, while its conditional target covariance is no greater. Thus, oracle prediction alignment preserves the population JiT objective up to a constant while providing a lower-variance training target. To make this construction practical with an imperfect EMA teacher, JAx combines ground-truth supervision with a reliability-gated coupling band that selects nearby teacher states based on prediction risk. On ImageNet 256x256, JAx consistently improves FID and accelerates convergence across JiT-B/16, L/16, and H/16, without an external encoder or changes to the architecture or sampling procedure. Gradient diagnostics further show reduced minibatch gradient variance, while ablations demonstrate that the gains cannot be explained by time reweighting alone. These results show that prediction-space supervision provides a simple and principled alternative to representation alignment for pixel-space generative models.

</details>


### [34] [HAWK: Rethinking Multimodal Drafting for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00623)
*Wenhan Yang,Anirudh Rao,Ashwin Chandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAWK是一种针对大视觉语言模型(LVLMs)的推测解码框架，解决了两个关键问题：1) 轻量级drafter难以利用丰富的多模态信息；2) 标准蒸馏仅监督drafter沿原始训练轨迹，忽略了drafter自身提议后目标预测的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码在LLMs中取得了显著的加速效果，但在LVLMs中效果不佳，原因包括：1) 轻量级drafter无法有效利用丰富的多模态信息；2) 标准蒸馏只沿原始训练轨迹监督drafter，未建模drafter提议后目标预测如何变化，导致后续步骤一致性下降。

Method: 提出HAWK方法：1) 使用表示相似性选择信息丰富的目标层并学习如何组合其隐藏状态；2) 为drafter提供来自目标模型的压缩视觉隐藏状态而非原始视觉token；3) 训练drafter捕捉目标预测在drafter提议后的变化，提高多步解码时的一致性。

Result: 在SmolVLM-256M上，HAWK在十个多模态基准测试中将平均接受长度从3.32提升至4.08（贪婪解码）/从2.89提升至3.41（采样），加速比从2.19x提升至2.60x（贪婪解码）/从1.92x提升至2.19x（采样），超越EAGLE-3。

Conclusion: HAWK通过表示相似性选择目标层并组合隐藏状态、提供压缩视觉信息、以及建模目标预测变化等创新，有效提升了LVLMs的推测解码效率，为多模态大模型的推理加速提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has achieved substantial lossless speedups for LLMs, but remains less effective for large vision-language models (LVLMs), where lightweight drafters struggle to use rich multimodal information. A second limitation is that standard distillation supervises the drafter only along the original training trajectory, without modeling how target predictions shift after the drafter's own proposals. As drafting moves away from this trajectory, the drafter can increasingly disagree with the target, reducing acceptance in later steps. We propose HAWK to address both limitations. HAWK uses representation similarity to select informative target layers and learns how to combine their hidden states. For visual information, it directly provides the drafter with compressed visual hidden states from the target model instead of raw visual tokens, making the visual information easier for a shallow drafter to use. HAWK also trains the drafter to capture how target predictions change after its own proposals, improving its agreement with the target during multi-step drafting. On SmolVLM-256M across ten multimodal benchmarks, HAWK raises average acceptance length from 3.32 to 4.08 and speedup from 2.19x to 2.60x over EAGLE-3 under greedy decoding, and from 2.89 to 3.41 and 1.92x to 2.19x under sampling.

</details>


### [35] [VisionQ: VLM-as-a-Judge Taxonomy, Dataset and Benchmark for Qualitative Analysis in Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00666)
*Vu Dinh Xuan,Duc-Hai Nguyen,Minh-Dung Dao,Vu Quynh Giao,Quang Hong Nguyen,Binh-Son Hua,Barry O'Sullivan,David Murphy,Hoang D. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisionQ是首个基于同行评审CV比较图的基准测试，通过将每个判断锚定在命名视觉标准上，评估视觉-语言模型在定性比较中的视觉推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评分标量质量或整体偏好，评判者可能因错误的视觉原因而被奖励选择首选图像，缺乏对实际视觉推理的评估。

Method: 构建包含1,409篇CVPR/ICCV论文、1,800+比较图和3,911个标注数据点的语料库；设计6轴51叶的视觉标准分类法；开发隐藏方法名称、标题和论文身份的条件化评估协议；训练基于Gemma-4-E4B的DPO调优VisionQ-Judge模型。

Result: VisionQ-Judge相比基线减少7.0pp的最后选项预测，提高2.5pp准确率；20个开源/闭源VLM评判器最佳仅达63.1%准确率（随机基线32.2%），各标准间可靠性差异显著。

Conclusion: VisionQ为首个基于同行评审比较图的条件化视觉判别基准，最佳VLM仅达63.1%准确率，表明该任务具有挑战性且VLM在视觉推理标准上存在明显局限性。

Abstract: Qualitative comparison figures are central evidence in computer vision papers, and vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to judge them. Yet existing benchmarks score only scalar quality or overall preference, so a judge can be rewarded for picking the preferred image for the wrong visual reason.
