### [212] [CompMat-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents for Computational Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00636)
*Chenmu Zhang,Levi Felix,Jun-Jie Zhang,Xingfu Li,Xuelian Jiang,Tao Jiang,Subhendu Mishra,Xixi Qin,Boris Yakobson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents on scientific research tasks is constrained by the time and resources required for the underlying experiments or calculations. In computational materials research, repeating the same expensive simulations across agents and trials can make evaluation impractical. We introduce CompMat-Bench, a benchmark of 94 tasks derived from recently published computational materials studies, each asking agents to complete a step toward achieving the study's scientific goal. We reproduce the research steps in advance and assess agents on preparing inputs and analyzing outputs for expensive simulations, so expensive simulations can be avoided during evaluation. The reproduced inputs and results serve as ground truth for grading agents with fixed rules, without an LLM judge. The benchmark supports four evaluation conditions: single tasks and workflows composed of related tasks, each with full or reduced methodological guidance. With full guidance on single tasks, agents based on three LLMs demonstrate the ability to complete individual materials research steps, with pass rates of 66.0-90.4% across 94 tasks. Both longer workflows and reduced guidance can limit agent performance, but in different ways for different agents: they lower the pass rates of the weaker agents, whereas the strongest agent falls only when a long workflow is combined with reduced guidance. Failure analysis attributes most failures to scientific errors rather than to errors in software usage. CompMat-Bench provides a basis for comparing agents on the steps of real materials research and for analyzing agent failure modes.

</details>


### [213] [Incident-Arena: Getting agents to the last nine of reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00648)
*Andre Fu,Malik Drabla,Leon Liu,Meji Abidoye,Marek Suppa,Lata Mishra,Adnan El Assadi,Yiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Incident-Arena基准测试，专门评估AI编码代理在生产事故响应（代理式SRE）中的能力。该基准包含20个基于真实开源软件的任务，在临时Kubernetes集群中部署生产应用并注入配置层和底层镜像故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI编码代理在应用开发中广泛应用，但在生产事故响应方面的能力却缺乏关注。现有基准测试存在局限性：使用不现实的玩具仓库、非标准框架实现和简单的静态验证器。

Method: 构建包含20个任务的Incident-Arena基准，每个任务部署生产应用到临时Kubernetes集群，从配置层到底层镜像注入故障，并提供持续的负载配置文件。提出超越静态检查的功能验证方法，确保系统级指标稳定同时保证修复安全性。

Result: 代理试验平均消耗2.81M tokens和41轮交互，展示了代理的长时序推理能力。在20个任务和3种应用 substrate上，前沿模型得分低于64.3%，失败原因包括诊断/定位错误、不完整修复和不安全回归。

Conclusion: 当前前沿AI模型在代理式SRE任务上表现不佳，需要改进诊断定位、完整修复和安全性方面的能力。Incident-Arena为评估AI代理在真实生产环境事故响应中提供了更现实的基准。

Abstract: AI coding agents are ubiquitous in engineering workflows amongst industry and academia. Yet, despite their use in app coding, relatively less attention has been paid to their ability to execute on production incident response. This emerging field, termed agentic site-reliability-engineering (SRE) contains benchmarks limited by (1) unrealistic environments, typically toy repositories (2) non-standard framework implementations and (3) simple static verifiers. We introduce Incident-Arena, a human-built benchmark of 20 carefully selected tasks grounded in real-world deployed open source software. Each task deploys a production application to an ephemeral Kubernetes cluster, injecting a fault from the config layer through underlying images, and a sustained load profile given the task requirements. We also present a novel verification method, going beyond static checks to functional verifiers, holding systems level metrics stable, while ensuring repairs are done safely. Agent trials run an average of 2.81M tokens and 41 turns, going beyond existing benchmarks, demonstrating agentic long horizon reasoning. Across 20 tasks and 3 application substrates, frontier models score below 64.3%, with failures extending from diagnosis/localization errors, through incomplete repairs and unsafe regressions.

</details>


### [214] [Agent Evaluation Reliability: More Tasks Won't (Always) Fix An Agent Leaderboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00651)
*Michael Hardy,Ruhana Azam,Anka Reuel,Mykel Kochenderfer,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究Agent评估中LLM排名可靠性问题，发现评估条件（如scaffolds或任务）会影响排名，提出贝叶斯方差分解框架区分信号与噪声，并给出评估设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agent评估用于比较LLM并指导部署决策，但排名可能受评估条件影响而非模型本身，因此需要确定哪些结论是可靠支持的。

Method: 开发贝叶斯方差分解框架处理稀疏、不平衡的Agent排行榜，分离信号（与声称相关的性能差异）和噪声（可改变排名的不相关变化），应用于22个基准测试。

Result: 固定模型-scaffold系统排名可靠(0.935-0.994)，但底层模型可靠性较低(0.148-0.841)；Scaffold选择可改变结论；即使无限多类似任务，模型排名可靠性最多提高0.097；汇集基准可提高跨任务排名可靠性从0.44到0.75，成本降低83%。

Conclusion: 可靠性取决于测量目标；评估设计应遵循声称意图：识别分数含义、诊断可靠性限制、将评估预算花在关键不确定性来源上。

Abstract: Agent evaluations are increasingly used to compare LLMs and inform deployment decisions, yet ranks can reflect not only the model but also the effects of the evaluation conditions such as the scaffolds or tasks. This makes reliability claim-dependent: an evaluation that reliably ranks deployed systems may not reliably rank underlying models. We ask which conclusions current agent evaluations reliably support and what additional evaluation would improve them. We develop a Bayesian variance-decomposition framework for sparse, imbalanced agent leaderboards and apply it to 22 benchmarks from the Holistic Agent Leaderboard and Harbor Index. The framework separates signal, performance differences relevant to the intended claim, from noise, irrelevant variation that can still change rankings. We find: (1) Reliability depends on the measurement goal. Fixed model-scaffold systems are ranked reliably (0.935-0.994), while underlying-model reliability is substantially lower (0.148-0.841). (2) Scaffold choice can change conclusions. Inter-scaffold reliability measures whether scaffolds preserve model rankings, showing that scaffold effects vary substantially across evaluations. (3) More tasks cannot resolve all uncertainty. Even infinitely many similarly constructed tasks improve model-ranking reliability of a benchmark by at most 0.097 when uncertainty is dominated by limited scaffold coverage. (4) Pooling diverse benchmarks can improve cross-task rankings at lower cost. For rankings across diverse agentic tasks, pooling benchmarks raises projected reliability from 0.44 to 0.75 at the same task budget and can reduce projected cost by up to 83\%. Evaluation design should follow the intended claim: identify what a score or ranking should mean, diagnose what limits its reliability, and spend evaluation budget on the sources of uncertainty that matter.

</details>


### [215] [When More Data Is Not Enough: The Context-Sufficiency Frontier in Generative AI Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00654)
*Merieme Askour,Ayoub Merimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出上下文充分性理论，解决生成式AI个性化中上下文信息过载问题，通过四状态模型识别最小相关上下文集，在家居零售推荐实验中验证相关上下文提升适当性，不相关上下文降低适当性并破坏检索稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机：生成式AI扩展了个性化能力，允许供应商在响应产生时提供变化的情境信息，但过多的上下文可能导致效果下降，需要建立理论框架指导上下文的选择。

Method: 研究方法：开发上下文充分性理论框架，识别四种状态（不充分、充分、饱和、干扰），引入上下文充分性前沿概念；在大规模家居零售商处进行生成式推荐系统的全因子实验。

Result: 实验结果：相关上下文显著提升推荐适当性，而不相关上下文不仅降低适当性，还导致检索结果不稳定。框架将个性化从"供给更多上下文"转向"识别当前交互实际需求并在服务过程中执行约束"。

Conclusion: 研究结论：框架将个性化范式从"供给更多上下文"转变为"识别当前交互实际需求并在服务过程中强制执行约束"，强调上下文的 relevance（相关性）比 volume（体积）更重要。

Abstract: Personalization has long relied on customer data to infer what an individual is likely to value. We call this customer evidence: the customer's historical behavior and preferences. Generative AI extends personalization by allowing providers to supply changing situational information at the moment a response is produced, without encoding every condition in advance. We define this provider-side context as information about what is possible, permitted, or advisable now. This flexibility creates a new problem: once context becomes easy to supply, more is not necessarily better. We develop a theory of context sufficiency in which the relevance of context to the customer's current intent matters more than its volume. The theory identifies four states, insufficiency, sufficiency, saturation, and interference, and introduces the Context-Sufficiency Frontier to locate the minimal relevant set. In a full-factorial experiment with a generative recommender at a large home-furnishing retailer, relevant context improved appropriateness, while irrelevant context reduced it and destabilized retrieval. The framework shifts personalization from supplying more context toward identifying what the current interaction actually requires and enforcing constraints throughout the service process.

</details>


### [216] [Backdoor Containment via Expert Quarantine and Shutdown in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00663)
*Jianwei Li,Min-Seon Kim,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种针对大型语言模型后门攻击的防御策略"学习但引导"(learn, but channel)，通过在训练时将后门行为吸引到隔离的专家组件中，部署时只需关闭该组件即可实现防御，方法称为QES。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御策略分为两类：抑制后门学习或先学习后净化。作者提出第三种策略——学习但引导，即允许后门形成但将其路由到隔离的可禁用组件中。

Method: 提出QES方法，在Transformer语言模型中增加路由专家特定LoRA分支和轻量级路由器，使用辅助路由目标将触发条件行为吸引到指定专家，同时保留其他部分的良性能力。部署时只需将隔离专家的路由权重置零。

Result: 在两个任务、三种攻击、四种模型家族上，攻击成功率从100%降至0-10%，同时下游效用得以保留或仅轻微影响。

Conclusion: 确立了"学习但引导"作为生成式LLM后门防御的新范式，提供了一种计算高效的containment策略。

Abstract: Backdoored large language models (LLMs) can behave normally on benign inputs while producing attacker-specified outputs under hidden triggers. Existing defenses span four stages--prior-training, in-training, post-training, and inference-time--and share one of two underlying strategies: either suppress backdoor learning (by filtering poisoned data or interrupting its acquisition during optimization) or learn, then purify (by repairing model weights or gating inputs after a fully backdoored model has formed). We propose a third strategy, learn, but channel: allow backdoor formation during training but route it into a designated, quarantined component that can be disabled at deployment. To this end, we propose Quarantined Expert Shutdown QES, a computationally efficient containment strategy built in a regularization-steered MoE-like setting. Specifically, given a poisoned dataset, QES augments a Transformer-based language model with routed expert-specific LoRA branches and lightweight routers, and uses auxiliary routing objectives to attract trigger-conditioned behavior into a designated expert while preserving benign capability elsewhere. At deployment, mitigation reduces to a single constant-time operation: zeroing the quarantined expert's routing weight, without trigger screening or further updating model weights. Empirically, our methods reduce the attack success rate ASR from 100% to 0-10% on most settings across two tasks, three attacks, and four model families, while downstream utility is often preserved or only modestly affected. These results establish learn, but channel as a previously unexplored regime for backdoor containment in generative LLMs.

</details>


### [217] [A Simple Doxastic Deontic Logic for Norm-Guided Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00668)
*Thorsten Engesser,Agata Ciabattoni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种简单的doxastic义务逻辑，用于处理自主系统在规范冲突、信息不完整或不可靠情况下的决策问题。该逻辑结合了Chellas最小义务逻辑的可经典简化片段、显式条件规范以及多主体KD45信念逻辑，并在此基础上定义了doxastic规范遵从优化问题，区分了主观优化（相对于主体信念）和客观优化（相对于实际事实）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统在面临冲突规范和不完整或不可靠信息时做出决策是一个根本性挑战。现有逻辑难以同时处理信念和规范推理。

Method: 结合Chellas最小义务逻辑的可经典简化片段、显式条件规范与多主体KD45信念逻辑（使规范依赖于主体对事实和规范的信念），定义了规范遵从优化问题。

Result: 给出了主观优化与客观优化何时一致的条件，并证明最优决策可在多项式时间内归约为加权部分MaxSAT。

Conclusion: 该框架为自主系统在规范冲突和信息不确定环境下的决策提供了形式化基础，且在特定条件下可高效求解。

Abstract: Making decisions despite conflicting norms and incomplete or unreliable information is a fundamental challenge for autonomous systems. We introduce a simple doxastic deontic logic for this setting: a classically reducible fragment of Chellas' Minimal Deontic Logic, extended with explicit conditional norms and combined with multi-agent KD45, so that norms can depend on agents' beliefs about both facts and norms. On this logic we define the Doxastic Norm Compliance Optimization Problem, where an agent chooses a decision minimizing weighted norm violations. We distinguish subjective optimization (relative to the agent's beliefs) from objective optimization (relative to the actual facts). We give conditions under which (i) the two coincide and (ii) optimal decision-making can be reduced to weighted partial MaxSAT in polynomial time.

</details>


### [218] [Ontology-Grounded, Reasoner-Verified Benchmarks for Evaluating LLM Reasoning in Scientific AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00682)
*Nishtha N. Vaidya,Stephan Grimm,Thomas Hubauer,Thomas A. Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动生成 ontology-grounded 多选题基准的 pipeline，用于评估大型语言模型在结构化知识上的逻辑推理能力。该方法从 OWL 2 本体生成 MCQ，正确答案由本体保证正确，干扰项通过扰动类定义公理的右侧类表达式生成，并经 OWL reasoner 形式化验证错误性。在三个本体（Pizza、PMDco、DOID）上生成了 112、2491 和 15216 道题目，零样本测试的六个 LLM 准确率为 41.1%-76.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 在科学 AI 应用中推理能力不足，常产生事实性错误，且可靠评估困难。人工构建数据集成本高，LLM 生成可能嵌入待测缺陷。需要高质量基准测试，既包含正确也包含错误的标记示例，并基于显式背景知识、可通过标准 reasoner 形式化验证。

Method: 提出自动生成 ontology-grounded MCQ 基准的 pipeline：1) 从任意充分公理化的 OWL 2 本体生成多选题；2) 正确答案由本体设计保证；3) 干扰项通过扰动类定义公理的右侧类表达式生成；4) 干扰项错误性由 OWL reasoner 通过 entailment 检查形式化验证。干扰项涵盖四类语义类别：从类不可满足到弱化的包含关系。

Result: 在三个本体上生成题目：Pizza（112道）、PMDco（2491道）、DOID（15216道）。干扰项覆盖四类语义错误。题目符合自然语言质量标准：LLM judge 平均得分 4.02、4.36、3.36（满分5），正确答案与干扰项相似度高于0.8。六个 LLM 零样本测试准确率 41.1%-76.8%，显著高于 25% 随机猜测基线，证明基准具有挑战性和区分度。

Conclusion: 该工作 Towards 更可靠的逻辑推理评估基准迈出了重要一步，为科学 AI 中 LLM 逻辑推理能力的诊断评估提供了新方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly underpin scientific AI applications that reason over structured knowledge, from biomedical question answering to materials informatics. However, their logical reasoning often falls short, producing factual inaccuracies unacceptable in these settings. Reliable evaluation remains challenging: manual dataset construction scales poorly, and LLM-based generation risks embedding the very flaws it aims to measure. High-quality benchmarks must ground both correct and incorrect labelled examples in explicit background knowledge, formally verifiable by a standard reasoner. We propose a pipeline that automatically generates ontology-grounded multiple-choice question (MCQ) benchmarks from any sufficiently axiomatised OWL 2 ontology, with correct answers grounded in the ontology by design. Distractors are generated by perturbing the right-hand-side class expressions of class definition axioms, and their incorrectness is formally verified by an OWL reasoner via entailment checks. We evaluate the pipeline on three ontologies: Pizza (small, academic), PMDco (complex, materials science), and DOID (large, biomedical), generating 112, 2,491, and 15,216 MCQs respectively. Distractors span four semantic categories from class unsatisfiability to weakened subsumptions, enabling diagnostic evaluation of specific reasoning failures. Items meet natural language quality standards: mean LLM judge scores of 4.02, 4.36, and 3.36 out of 5 confirm fluency, and correct-answer-to-distractor similarity above 0.8 shows that wrong options cannot be dismissed on surface form alone. Six LLMs evaluated zero-shot achieve 41.1-76.8% accuracy, well above the 25% random-guessing baseline, indicating the benchmarks are challenging and discriminative. This work is a step towards more reliable benchmarks for assessing logical reasoning in scientific AI.

</details>


### [219] [Backdoor Purification for LoRA-Tuned LLMs via Null-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00685)
*Jianwei Li,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需先验知识、干净参考或重训练的 LoRA 微调 LLM 后门净化方法，通过构建输入输出通道的正交空域并将 LoRA 更新投影到该空域来消除后门，可将攻击成功率从近100%降至10%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门净化方法通常依赖强假设（触发器先验知识、干净参考访问或激进重训练），且缺乏全面评估，严重限制了其实际应用范围。因此需要一种无需这些假设甚至无需可疑参数后验重训练的后门净化方法。

Method: 通过精心设计的数据 curation 和特征近似方法，提取高保真度的后门方向，然后为每一层或每个头在输入和输出通道上构建正交空域，将 LoRA 更新投影到该空域上。从文本分类设置中的单层逐步扩展到生成任务中的全参数 LLM。

Result: 实验表明，空域投影方法将攻击成功率（ASR）从接近100%降低到10%以下，同时有效保留了在下游任务适应期间习得的基座模型良性性能和适配器能力。

Conclusion: 提出的空域投影方法成功将攻击成功率从接近100%降低到10%以下，同时保持了模型的基础能力和下游任务学习能力。该方法无需后验重训练，具有较强的实际应用价值。

Abstract: With the rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, the risk of backdoor attacks has become more severe. Existing backdoor purification methods typically rely on at least one of the strong assumptions, such as prior knowledge of triggers, access to clean references, or aggressive retraining, and they often lack comprehensive evaluations. These constraints substantially limit their practical applicability. To overcome these challenges, our work proposes purifying LoRA-tuned LLMs without these assumptions and even without post-hoc retraining of the suspect parameters. Our objective is to significantly reduce the attack success rates (ASR) while preserving both (i) the base model's general capabilities and (ii) the new downstream skills learned through the adapter. Through a series of ablation studies, we progressively scale our approach from a single layer in a text classification setting to a full-parameter LLM in the generative task. Through careful data curation and feature approximation, we extract high-fidelity backdoor directions and, for each layer or head, construct orthogonal null spaces in both the input and output channels, onto which the LoRA updates are projected. Empirically, our null-space projection method reduces the ASR from nearly 100% to less than 10%, while preserving the base model's benign performance and the adapter's learned abilities during downstream task adaptation.

</details>


### [220] [R-GroundBench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for R-Group Groundingin Markush Molecular Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00700)
*Xin Wang,Zichuan Ying,Xinna Lin,Junqi Zhang,Hanyi Xiong,Tianyu Gao,Hairong Zhang,Qixiang Hua,Botian Shi,Zhenhailong Wang,Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了R-GroundBench基准，用于评估AI模型对专利Markush结构中R-group占位符的分子 grounding 能力，揭示了当前模型在分子识别与 grounding 之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子-语言基准测试关注完全指定的分子，而Markush结构中的R-group grounding基本未被评估，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了R-GroundBench基准，包含VQA多选题 tracks和开放生成tracks，设计了不同难度和模态分割，测试了多种化学VLMs和LLMs。

Result: 模型在简单VQA上准确率超90%，但困难VQA降至56-66%；化学领域VLMs在困难VQA上仅25.7-46.2%；生成任务精确匹配率低于20%，含视觉输入时低于8%。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统缺乏对Markush编辑的可靠 grounding 和执行能力，在科学发现中面临重大挑战。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI for scientific discovery enable molecular understandingand design, yet reasoning over incomplete chemical representations remainsunclear.Markush structures, which encode molecular families through variable R-groupplaceholders (\textit{{R\textsubscript{{1}}}}, \textit{{R\textsubscript{{2}}}}, \textit{{X}}, etc.), are ubiquitous in pharmaceutical patents and requiregrounding across molecular, textual, and chemical information.However, existing molecule-language benchmarks focus on fully specifiedmolecules, leaving R-group grounding largely unevaluated.We introduce R-GroundBench:, a diagnostic benchmark built from real patent Markushstructures, featuring a Multiple-Choice (VQA) track with controlled difficultyand modality splits, and an open-ended Generation track.Our results reveal a substantial gap between recognition andmolecular grounding.While models achieve over 90\% accuracy on Easy VQA, performance drops to56--66\% on Hard VQA when shortcuts are controlled.Chemical-domain VLMs also remain unreliable, achieving only 25.7--46.2\% on HardVQA despite domain-specific pretraining.Moreover, Generation Exact Match remains below 20\% for most models and below8\% when visual input is required.These findings reveal that current AI systems lack reliable grounding andexecution for Markush editing, highlighting challenges for AI-drivenscientific discovery.

</details>


### [221] [ReLiveGym: Evaluating Long-Lived Agents over Weeks of Replayed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00710)
*Xisen Jin,Jingheng Li,Zhenglun Chen,Junyi Du,Xiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReLiveGym是一个用于评估长时间运行任务的诊断环境，通过重放真实世界的新闻、市场和社交媒体流来模拟数周的稀疏行动。研究了模型选择和智能体设计如何影响长期任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时序智能体工作未充分考虑动态环境中的持续监控或周期性行动需求。现有任务通常在静态环境中进行，无法捕捉时间敏感性、推理强度和行动频率等挑战。

Method: 构建ReLiveGym诊断评估环境，模拟真实世界的新闻、市场和社交媒体流；跨8个基础语言模型评估；研究行动时机确定机制；评估从 hindsight 反馈中持续学习的效果。

Result: 行动时机机制成为长时序任务的重要设计轴；最优设计因任务和模型选择而异；持续学习可改善性能并解决失败模式。

Conclusion: 模型选择、行动时机机制和反馈使用是长时序智能体设计的重要考虑因素。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents become widely adopted, they are increasingly deployed for tasks that require persistent monitoring or recurring actions (e.g., market analysis). These agents are expected to operate unattended for days or weeks, act at the right timing, and adapt to the dynamic environment over time. These challenges are not fully captured in the existing long-horizon agent work, as they often consider a static environment that is not temporally changing. We introduce ReLiveGym, a diagnostic evaluation environment of long-lived tasks in which agents act sparsely over simulated weeks of chronologically replayed real-world news, market, and social-media streams. The tasks span diverse levels of time sensitivity, reasoning intensity, and recurrence. Across eight base language models, we investigate how model choice and harness design affect agent performance on such long-lived tasks. Our results show that how agents determine when to act arises as an important harness-design axis for long-lived tasks; and that the optimal design varies across tasks and sometimes model choices as well. We also evaluate how continuous learning from hindsight feedback affects performance and addresses failure modes observed in these long-lived tasks. These findings indicate model choice, action timing mechanism, and use of feedback as important considerations in the design of long-lived agents. Code: https://github.com/SaharaLabsAI/ReLiveGym

</details>


### [222] [Robust Nash Alignment under Preference Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00715)
*Shihab Ahmed,Debamita Ghosh,David Tang,Yudan Wang,Alvaro Velasquez,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出鲁棒纳什对齐框架，通过四玩家原对偶代理游戏处理不确定偏好，实现O(1/√T)收敛，为原始鲁棒目标提供近似最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于偏好的对齐方法对噪声、异构或部署后变化的成对偏好具有脆弱性，需要一个能处理不确定偏好的鲁棒框架。

Method: 提出四玩家原对偶代理游戏，包含领导者策略、跟随者策略、对抗核和对偶变量，采用单循环乐观镜像下降-上升算法求解，建立代理目标与截断硬约束目标之间的下界关系。

Result: 代理目标始终下界截断硬约束目标，量化了代理-硬间隙，证明了在精确条件下两者等价，实现了O(1/√T)平均迭代收敛。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了鲁棒纳什对齐框架来处理偏好不确定性，通过四玩家原对偶代理游戏和乐观镜像下降-上升算法实现了O(1/√T)收敛速度，在表格游戏和LLM对齐实验中验证了理论并提升了性能。

Abstract: Preference-based alignment methods typically optimize against a single preference model, and can therefore be brittle when pairwise preferences are uncertain: noisy, heterogeneous, or shift after deployment. To address these issues, we propose Robust Nash Alignment, a game-theoretic framework for alignment to uncertain pairwise preferences. Our formulation has a major learner seeking a policy with a large worst-case win rate against both an adversarial competitor and any preference kernel lying in an ambiguity set around a nominal preference. When the ambiguity set captures the uncertainty in preferences, the resulting robust objective of the game directly yields a certified lower bound on worst-case performance. However, we note this problem is computationally challenging to optimize, and to address this, we introduce a four-player primal-dual proxy game involving the leader policy, follower policy, adversarial kernel, and dual variable, and develop a single-loop optimistic mirror descent-ascent algorithm for it. We show that the proxy always lower-bounds the truncated hard-constrained objective, quantify the proxy-to-hard gap, and characterize an exactness condition under which the proxy recovers the robust objective. We then prove an \(\mathcal{{O}}(1/\sqrt{{T}})\) average-iteration convergence for the proxy-game duality gap, which implies a near-optimal robust policy for the original robust objective. Experiments on controlled tabular games and LLM alignment with uncertain preference further validate the convergence theory and show improved performance over nominal baselines.

</details>


### [223] [Enterprise Representation Simplification (ERS): Reducing Representational Complexity for Enterprise AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00791)
*Terry Dorsey,Kevin Huggins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出企业表示简化(ERS)概念，旨在减少不必要的表示复杂性，同时在定义范围内保留所需信息；并提出表示复杂性(ERC)模型，用于比较不同表示状态下的复杂性。ERC从表示对象、交互、行为和支持源四个维度表征表示范围，可在表示层和任务层进行定义，实现架构简化与检索优化的区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业信息通过应用、项目、技术、组织边界和本地需求形成的构件来表示，这些结构随时间积累产生表示复杂性，需要企业维护并由信息消费者和AI系统解释。因此需要一种方法来简化和量化这种复杂性。

Method: 提出ERC模型，从四个维度表征表示范围：表示对象、交互、行为（形成依赖类别）和支持源（表征表示暴露）。建立经济模型区分 recurring全局表示成本、recurring任务级成本和一次性转换成本。引用Text-to-SQL研究验证降低模式复杂性对提高推理准确性的作用。

Result: ERC作为表示中性模型可在表示层和任务层定义，区分架构简化与检索优化；经济模型可评估特定时间范围内的成本效益；降低任务级ERC可减少AI系统需要识别、关联和解释的表示范围。

Conclusion: ERC不是通用的复杂性、性能或成本指标，而是提供可测量的架构变量，用于比较表示替代方案、转换效果、经济结果和AI推理性能。为企业在信息架构优化和AI系统效率提升方面提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Enterprise information is represented through artifacts shaped by applications, projects, technologies, organizational boundaries, and local requirements. These structures accumulate over time, creating representational complexity that must be maintained by the enterprise and interpreted by information consumers and AI systems. This paper introduces Enterprise Representation Simplification (ERS) as reducing unnecessary representational complexity while preserving required information within a defined scope, and Enterprise Representation Complexity (ERC), a representation-neutral model for comparing complexity across representation states.
  ERC characterizes representational extent through four dimensions: Representation Objects, Interactions, Behaviors, and Supporting Sources. Objects, Interactions, and Behaviors form dependent categories, while Supporting Sources characterize representation exposure. ERC is defined at representation and task levels, enabling comparison and distinguishing architectural simplification from retrieval optimization.
  The paper develops two consequences of ERS. First, representational structures create lifecycle obligations for maintenance, governance, dependencies, change, enhancement, and operation. An economic model distinguishes recurring global representation cost, recurring task-level cost, and one-time transformation cost, enabling evaluation over a defined time horizon. Second, reductions in task-level ERC reduce the representational extent an AI system must identify, relate, and interpret. Text-to-SQL research provides evidence that reduced schema and reasoning complexity can improve reasoning accuracy.
  ERC is not a universal complexity, performance, or cost metric. It provides measurable architectural variables for comparing representational alternatives, transformation effects, economic outcomes, and AI reasoning performance.

</details>


### [224] [Sapien: A Stateful Policy Engine for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00797)
*Corinn Tiffany,Wen Zhang,Eugene Bagdasarian,Lillian Tsai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sapien是一种用于在AI代理工具调用上执行状态上下文策略的策略引擎，通过正则表达式扩展、延迟策略生成和作用域语义检查来防止代理执行恶意行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多步骤任务中，哪些操作有效通常取决于代理已经完成的行为和学到的内容。现有工具允许列表无法有效处理长时域任务中的上下文相关攻击，需要一种能够根据代理状态动态调整的安全防御机制。

Method: 设计Sapien策略引擎，使用扩展正则表达式指定允许的工具调用序列，包含状态谓词、延迟策略生成和作用域语义检查功能。

Result: Sapien在保持代理效用方面与无约束代理仅差几个百分点。即使代理被完全劫持，SAPien的策略也能阻止AgentDojo上93-95%的攻击和Toolathlon上62-85%的攻击（长时域任务中比工具允许列表多阻止2倍）。

Conclusion: 状态上下文策略引擎能有效平衡AI代理的安全性与实用性，为防止代理采取恶意行为提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Contextual security defenses prevent AI agents from taking rogue actions by synthesizing a task-specific policy and enforcing it on the agent's tool calls. In multi-step tasks, however, which actions are valid often depends on what the agent has already done and learned. We present Sapien, a policy engine for enforcing stateful contextual policies. A Sapien policy specifies permitted tool-call sequences using a regular expression extended with stateful predicates, deferred policy generation, and scoped semantic checks. We show that Sapien stays within a few percent of an unconstrained agent's utility. Even if the agent is fully hijacked, Sapien's policies rule out 93-95% of attacks on AgentDojo and 62-85% on Toolathlon (twice as many as tool allowlists on long-horizon tasks).

</details>


### [225] [Learning Multiple Timescales for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00849)
*Pedro Robles Dutenhefner,Dikshant Shehmar,Wagner Meira,Marlos C. Machado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GITA方法解决离线目标条件强化学习中长程任务的价值估计问题，通过聚合多个时间抽象尺度k的advantage-weighted监督信号来训练单一策略，避免了固定k值带来的局部分辨率与长程信号之间的权衡取舍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线目标条件强化学习方法在长程任务中表现不佳，因为折扣机制会缩小远距离状态间的价值差异，使其低于函数近似误差，导致智能体缺乏状态排序信号。时间抽象虽能恢复长程信号，但单一固定k值无法同时满足所有状态-目标距离的需求：大k值保留长程价值差异但模糊近距离状态区分，小k值反之。

Method: 提出广义隐式时间抽象(GITA)，该方法让单个价值函数以k为条件，通过跨多个k值聚合advantage-weighted监督信号来训练一个策略，其中对状态-目标对赋予更大正advantage的尺度k在更新中贡献更大。

Result: 在OGBench上，GITA相比HIQL提升平均成功率25个百分点(相对提升73%)，比最强固定k方法OTA提升7个百分点(相对提升14%)。

Conclusion: GITA无需在局部分辨率和长程信号之间做选择，兼顾两者且不局限于单一k值，在离线目标条件强化学习任务中显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Existing approaches to offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) struggle with long-horizon tasks. Discounting shrinks value differences between distant states until they fall below the function approximation error, leaving the agent with no signal for ranking states. Temporal abstraction, which treats k environment steps as a single transition, restores this signal at long range, but no single fixed k suits all state-goal distances: large k preserves value differences across long temporal distances while collapsing distinctions between nearby states, and small k does the reverse. We make this trade-off explicit and introduce Generalized Implicit Temporal Abstraction (GITA), which conditions a single value function on k. GITA trains one policy by aggregating advantage-weighted supervision across multiple k values, so scales assigning larger positive advantages to a state-goal pair contribute more strongly to its update. GITA does not need to choose between local resolution and long-range signal; it retains both without committing to a single k. On OGBench, GITA outperforms a broad range of offline GCRL baselines, raising average success rate across all tasks by 25 percentage points (73% relative improvement) over HIQL. It also improves over the strongest fixed-k method, OTA, by 7 percentage points (14% relative).

</details>


### [226] [An Educator-Guided LLM Pedagogical Agent for Scaffolded Feedback in Conceptual Database Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00870)
*Sara Riazi,Pedram Rooshenas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于LLM的教育辅导智能体，为概念数据库设计（ERD）提供分阶段脚手架式反馈。系统将反馈建立在学生作品、作业要求、教师评分标准和教学资源之上，采用四阶段工作流，从概念检查逐步推进到详细反馈和局部澄清。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决数据库设计教学中即时反馈的难题，传统的自动化反馈系统往往缺乏对学生作品的深入诊断能力，且难以控制反馈的形式和披露时机。该研究旨在通过隐藏诊断与分阶段披露机制，提供更灵活、更有效的教育反馈。

Method: 系统架构将隐藏的诊断模块与学生可见的工作流分离，实现为四阶段工作流：概念检查→引导应用→低细节反馈→局部澄清。每个反馈请求创建与版本化ERD状态关联的有状态会话。部署于三个ERD环境，收集383个反馈案例进行定量和定性分析。

Result: 71.1%的目标级变化部分或完全整合了隐藏的诊断目标，许多发生在第1-2阶段之后。定性分析显示分阶段披露有时会隐藏不准确细节、支持选择性吸收或允许后续修正，但部分误差仍影响了修改。调查反馈偏向延迟披露和学生自主性，但指出间接性和重复性问题。

Conclusion:  educator-guided LLM pedagogical agent能够有效提供分阶段脚手架式反馈，四阶段工作流设计在控制反馈形式和时机方面具有可行性，但需改进反馈的直接性和减少重复，以更好支持学生学习。

Abstract: We present an educator-guided LLM pedagogical agent for scaffolded feedback in conceptual database design. Integrated into an entity--relationship diagram (ERD) editor, the system grounds feedback in the student artifact, assignment requirements, educator-authored rubrics, and instructional resources. Its architecture separates hidden, artifact-grounded diagnosis from the workflow that controls the form and disclosure level of student-facing support.
  We instantiate the architecture as a four-stage workflow progressing from concept checks and guided application to low-detail feedback and localized clarification. Each feedback request creates a stateful episode linked to versioned ERD states. In a deployment spanning three ERD environments and 383 feedback episodes, 71.1\% of observed target-level changes fully or partially incorporated the hidden diagnostic target, including many after Stages~1--2. Qualitative analysis showed that staged disclosure sometimes withheld inaccurate details, supported selective uptake, or allowed later recovery, though some errors still shaped revisions. Survey responses from a self-selected sample favored delayed disclosure and student agency but noted indirectness and repetition.

</details>


### [227] [MemFit: Efficient Long-Term Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00872)
*Mitchell Piehl,Muchao Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemFit是一个面向对话代理的长效记忆系统，通过LLM-free的插入和检索策略，显著降低记忆操作的成本和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统依赖LLM代理进行记忆组织和整合，导致写入操作成本高、效率低。

Method: 1) 将每个对话轮次完整存储在只读append-only存储中，使用段落摘要进行索引，无需LLM即可快速插入；2) 采用LLM-free的多路径检索策略，结合词汇和语义信号，并对文本和多模态场景下的字幕增强片段进行跨编码器重排序。

Result: 在LoCoMo、MemGallery和LongMemEval-S三个基准测试上达到最优性能，记忆构建时间和成本降低数倍。

Conclusion: MemFit为持久化智能体记忆提供了可扩展且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-term memory systems for large language models (LLMs) have gained popularity for extending reasoning capabilities across applications. Current memory systems rely on LLM agents to organize and consolidate memory, resulting in costly, inefficient write operations. To address this limitation, we propose MemFit, a long-term memory system for conversational agents that reduces the cost and latency of memory operations. Unlike existing systems that rely on expensive LLM calls for memory construction or discard surface-level details through compression, MemFit stores each turn verbatim in an append-only store with near-instantaneous, LLM-free insertion, indexing turns with segment summaries rather than replacing them. Additionally, MemFit uses an LLM-free, multi-path retrieval strategy that combines lexical and semantic signals with cross-encoder reranking over caption- augmented episodes in both textual and multimodal settings. Empirical results on three widely used benchmarks, LoCoMo, MemGallery, and LongMemEval-S, show that MemFit achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing memory construction time and cost several-fold, providing a scalable and efficient solution for persistent agentic memory.

</details>


### [228] [OR for AI That Does OR: Routing LLMs up the Escalator inside the OSCAR Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00912)
*Jinzhi Bu,Haixin Tang,Huanan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSCAR是一个利用LLM进行优化建模的框架,通过离线模拟器验证候选模型,即使找到可行解也会继续搜索更好的方案。该框架在五个基准问题上达到95-100%准确率,成本远低于Claude Code和Codex。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM将业务描述转换为优化模型时,可能误解约束或目标,导致求解器给出"最优解但非正确问题"的解。即使解满足操作规则,也存在更优方案。需要低成本、可本地部署的准确建模框架。

Method: 开发OSCAR框架,使用离线模拟器(经标记决策示例验证)比较候选模型,将寻找下一个经认证改进的过程建模为序贯决策。在简化已知先验设置下证明成本有序升级最优,对一般菜单给出先验无关的竞争保证。使用两个小型开源LLM(单GPU可部署)进行实验。

Result: OSCAR在五个基准问题上达到95-100%准确率。两个小LLM单次尝试准确率平均为29%和48%。Codex和Claude Code的平均token成本分别是OSCAR的3.1倍和5.8倍。

Conclusion: OSCAR支持开源模型本地或云端部署,成本效益显著。企业应维护标记的可行/不可行决策示例来澄清操作规则,并在LLM解释与规则冲突时遵循这些标签。随着LLM能力和价格变化,OSCAR的简单规则和可调设置有助于企业适应模型选择并从中获益。

Abstract: Large language models can translate business descriptions into optimization models, but executable code may misrepresent constraints or objectives. A solver can then return an optimal solution to the wrong problem. Even when the solution satisfies the intended operating rules, a better plan may exist. For organizations that repeatedly use optimization modeling, an LLM-based framework should produce accurate formulations at low cost and, ideally, run locally. We study how to verify improvements and allocate attempts across LLMs that differ in price and capability. We develop OSCAR (Optimization modeling by Simulator, Coder, And Reviewer), which uses an offline Simulator certified against labeled decision examples to compare candidates and continues searching beyond feasibility. We model the search for the next certified improvement as sequential decisions under unobserved difficulty: which LLMs to call and when to stop. In a simplified known-prior setting, we give conditions under which cost-ordered escalation is optimal. For general menus, we derive a prior-free competitive guarantee. On five benchmark problems, OSCAR achieves 95% to 100% accuracy at the reported settings using two small open-weight LLMs, each deployable locally on a single GPU. Their single-attempt accuracies average 29% and 48%. In five runs per problem, Codex and Claude Code incur average token costs 3.1 and 5.8 times OSCAR's, respectively. OSCAR supports open-weight models locally or in the cloud, depending on budget and confidentiality requirements. Firms should maintain labeled decision examples of feasible and infeasible decisions to clarify plain-language operating rules. OSCAR follows these labels when an LLM's interpretation conflicts with them. As LLM capabilities and prices change, OSCAR's simple operating rules and adjustable settings help firms adapt their model choices and benefit from these advances.

</details>


### [229] [Finding the Right Fit: Model-Harness Interactions across Agent Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00917)
*Yixuan Li,Yiyun Zhou,Yao Long Teng,Fuchao Yang,Yanchen Deng,Zhiyi Lyu,Xuyu Dong,Feng Chen,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了66种智能体系统配置（语言模型+工具链），发现模型排名会随工具链变化而反转，强调模型、工具链和任务需要联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强模型、强工具链或其组合在不同设置下是否保持优势，解决智能体系统中模型与工具链适配性问题

Method: 在TUA-Bench、ALE-CLI和Terminal-Bench 4上测试4种工具链（OpenHands、DeepSeek Harness、PI、openJiuwen）与5种模型的66种配置组合

Result: 模型排名随工具链变化而反转：Terminal-Bench 4上，Claude在OpenHands中领先GPT 7.94分，但在PI中落后30.16分；5个模型中4个的最佳工具链随基准改变；openJiuwen+Kimi组合在三个基准上均获最高分；GPT在PI工具链下得分高于DSH且成本低四分之三

Conclusion: 模型、工具链和任务应联合评估；工具链如何返回失败信息影响模型修复效果；不同模型适合不同工具链（如GPT适合PI的精简架构，Kimi适合openJiuwen）

Abstract: Choosing an agent system means choosing both a language model and the harness through which it acts. We ask whether a strong model, harness, or pairing stays strong when the setting changes. We evaluate 66 configurations: four configurable harnesses (OpenHands, DeepSeek Harness, PI, and openJiuwen) paired with five models on TUA-Bench, ALE-CLI, and Terminal-Bench 4, plus the native Codex-GPT and Claude Code-Claude pairings. Model rankings reverse across harnesses. On Terminal-Bench 4, Claude leads GPT by 7.94 points in OpenHands but trails it by 30.16 points in PI. For four of the five models, the best harness changes from one benchmark to another, yet some pairings hold: openJiuwen gives Kimi its highest score on all three benchmarks, by 5.61 to 11.11 points. A model's own vendor harness is not reliably its best, and higher cost does not reliably buy a higher score. On Terminal-Bench 4, GPT scores higher under PI than under DSH at less than a quarter of the cost per task. Matched trajectories suggest why fit varies. Models start almost all repairs themselves, so much depends on whether the harness hands failures back in a form the model can use. GPT does best with PI's lean scaffold, while Kimi, which often issues malformed tool calls, does best in openJiuwen. We argue that the model, the harness, and the task should be evaluated together, and we release the harness adapters, evaluation code, and all 6,204 scored trajectories at https://github.com/liyix/finding-the-right-fit and https://huggingface.co/datasets/yixuanli97/finding-the-right-fit.

</details>


### [230] [ABDA-NL: A Natural-Language Scenario Explorer for Argument-Based Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00947)
*Shawn Bowers,Martin Caminada,Haoyang Liu,Bertram Ludäscher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ABDA-NL为基于ASPIC知识库的论证讨论系统ABDA添加自然语言接口，使用大型语言模型作为语言与形式化之间的桥梁，让用户可通过自然语言查看论证状态、探索讨论游戏、进行假设分析、提问和创作新规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始ABDA系统虽然能进行基于论证的讨论，但缺乏用户友好的自然语言界面，用户难以直观理解论证过程和结果，也无法方便地探索假设场景或进行交互式推理。

Method: 引入大型语言模型实现：(1)从文档和场景状态回答用户问题；(2)将自然语言编辑翻译为形式化陈述；(3)所有模型输出需经用户验证确认后才生效，保持ABDA引擎作为唯一权威。

Result: 用户可查看结论的接受/拒绝/未决状态；打开交互式讨论游戏了解原因；通过挂起假设/规则或改变偏好探索"如果"场景；用自然语言提问并获得文档答案；用英文创作新事实、假设和规则。

Conclusion: ABDA-NL成功在保持ABDA引擎确定性权威的同时，大幅提升了系统的可访问性和交互性，使非专业用户也能方便地使用基于论证的推理系统。

Abstract: ABDA-NL adds a natural-language interface to ABDA, a system for argument-based discussion using ASPIC- knowledge bases under grounded semantics. Users see which conclusions are accepted, rejected, or undecided, open an interactive rendering of the grounded discussion game to learn why, explore what-if alternatives by suspending assumptions and rules or changing preferences, ask questions that are answered from a scenario's reference documents, and author new facts, assumptions, and rules in plain English. A large language model provides the bridge between language and formalism: it answers questions from the documents and the current state of the scenario, and it translates plain-English edits into candidate formal statements. The deterministic ABDA engine remains the sole source of arguments, attacks, and acceptance labels, and every proposal of the model is validated and confirmed by the user before it takes effect.

</details>


### [231] [PG-SFT: Balancing Capability Acquisition and Retention in Offline Agent Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00949)
*Ronghua Li,Zi Liang,Zhishan Li,Shinan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在智能体轨迹监督微调(SFT)中，如何平衡获取新能力与保留现有能力之间的权衡。标准SFT虽然提升目标基准，但会损害其他能力。PG-SFT方法利用轨迹的逐回合信息增益作为指示器来调整监督强度，在保持非目标任务性能方面取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机：当前在智能体轨迹上进行监督微调是训练专用工具使用智能体的标准方法，但强制模型逐token模仿推理和动作会损害基础模型的其他能力（如通用推理、工具调用、代码生成）。如何平衡获取新能力与保留现有能力是一个重要问题。

Method: 研究方法：1）比较多种基线方法（标准SFT、KL惩罚、限制更新幅度等）；2）提出特权引导SFT（PG-SFT），利用智能体轨迹的回合级信息增益作为指标来调整监督强度。

Result: 研究结果：标准SFT提升目标基准但降低多个非目标基准分数；KL惩罚和限制更新幅度未能避免这种回归趋势；PG-SFT在评估基准上产生更有利的权衡，显著减少分布漂移和广泛能力退化，但以目标任务性能轻微下降为代价。

Conclusion: 结论：平衡获取-保留权衡不仅取决于模型是否锚定其基础行为，还取决于监督应该从该行为偏离的程度和位置。PG-SFT通过调整不同回合的监督强度来更好地处理这一权衡。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on offline agent trajectories is the standard approach for training specialized tool-using agents, but forcing models to imitate reasoning and actions token by token may harm other capabilities (e.g., general reasoning, tool calling, code generation) of the base model. In this work, we focus on studying \emph{{how to better balance the trade-off between acquiring new capabilities and preserving existing ones during agent trace SFT}}. By comparing several baselines in our setup, standard SFT improves the target benchmark while lowering several non-target benchmark scores; meanwhile, simply constraining distributional drift using KL penalty or limiting the update magnitude did not avoid this regression trend. Motivated by recent token-wise adaptive learning objectives, this work proposes \textbf{{Privilege-Guided SFT (PG-SFT)}} to leverage turn-level information gain of agent trajectories as an indicator to adjust supervision strength. PG-SFT yields a more favorable observed trade-off on the evaluated benchmarks, substantially reducing distributional drift and broad capability degradation at the cost of slight degradation in target-task performance. Our findings suggest that balancing the acquisition--retention trade-off depends not only on whether the model is anchored to its base behavior, but also on where and how strongly supervision should depart from that behavior.}}

</details>


### [232] [Cybernetic and Epistemic: A Missing Vocabulary for Trustworthy Agentic Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00961)
*Jérémie Lumbroso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 随着AI系统越来越多地承担代码生成任务，监督AI代码生成的挑战日益凸显。当前CS教育研究中存在词汇鸿沟：将"人类监督"混用了两种不同性质的语言功能——控制行动的语言（控制论意义上的成功标准是现实匹配语言）和协调认知的语言（认知论意义上的成功标准是语言符合现实且可核查）。论文提出：当前的问题在于用认知形式的语言做控制论的工作——产出符合审批要求但非真相的解释。论文提出治理准则：每个重大决策都应附带"若条件不同会如何选择"的条件声明，使第三方可验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代码生成从"写代码"转变为"监督写代码"，但缺乏区分两种监督语言的词汇表。当前监督只检查输出是否被批准，容易沦为橡皮图章；而要求对代理问责则需要其推理过程可追溯和可核查。CS教育研究需要新的监督框架。

Method: 论文呈现三个 delegation 案例说明认知参与的可操作性，一个公开记录中建议按自身条款撤回的案例，以及一个无需理由的裁量选择导致的失败案例。提出治理准则：每个重大选择应包含"若条件不同会如何"的反向条件声明。给出可操作形式——两部分重建测试（评估第二个读者是否能预测扰动下的行为）和ORRCF deliberation-recording convention。

Result: 提出治理标准：每个重大决策需附带可由第三方检验的条件声明，否则第三方无法区分真实决策与橡皮图章。通过ORRCF约定将条件声明设为记录选择的必选项。

Conclusion: 现有技术信任属性之外，需要增加治理属性：每项重大选择必须附带可检验的反向条件声明（若条件X不成立则会做Y）。这使得监督从"检查是否批准"转向"验证推理是否可辩护"，为代理系统治理提供了可操作的测试框架。

Abstract: As code generation is increasingly delegated to AI systems, the bottleneck is shifting from writing code to supervising the systems that write it --- a shift CS-education researchers have begun to name. This shift exposes a vocabulary gap: the field asks for "human oversight" without a working distinction between the two things language does in a delegation channel --- coordinate action (cybernetic: words succeed when the world comes to match them) and coordinate understanding (epistemic: they succeed when they answer to the world and a hearer can check that they do). The failure this names is not cybernetic language but epistemic-form language doing cybernetic work: explanation-shaped output calibrated for approval rather than truth. Oversight that checks only whether an output was approved is satisfiable by rubber-stamping; oversight that holds an agent accountable requires the reasoning behind its work be retrievable and checkable. We present three delegation episodes --- illustrations, not controlled evidence --- in which epistemic engagement proved practicable while remaining auditable, one public record where a recommendation was withdrawn on its own stated terms, and one failure case illustrating oversight that requires no reasons for its discretionary choices. We propose a criterion for agentic-system governance, alongside existing technical trust properties: every consequential choice should carry the condition under which it would have gone otherwise, in a form a third party can test. Without such a condition, a third party cannot distinguish a decision from a rubber stamp. We give the criterion an operational form --- a two-part reconstruction test scoring a delegation record by whether a second reader can predict what the agent does under a perturbation --- and a deliberation-recording convention, ORRCF, that makes the condition a required component of every recorded choice.

</details>


### [233] [RISED: RubrIcs for agentic multi-environment Selection and sElf-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00979)
*Jingtan Wang,Sirajul Salekin,Young mok Jung,Javier Movellan,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Manjot Bilkhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RISED方法，通过使用预定义的评价标准(rubrics)来指导LLM智能体在多样化交互环境中的训练，解决传统基于标量奖励的多环境强化学习的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在跨环境训练中存在两个主要问题：1)课程学习和数据选择策略仅在环境层面分配训练资源，未考虑跨环境的 rollout 关系；2)当环境中全是成功或全是失败的 rollout 时，缺乏组内相对奖励信号。这两个问题凸显了单纯依赖标量奖励的局限性。

Method: 1) 使用LLM评判器根据预定义的 rubric 词汇表对每个 rollout 进行标注；2) 基于标注结果指导在线数据选择，选择与混合环境批次整体行为组成一致且与已选数据不重复的数据；3) 利用正向 rubrics 作为特权上下文进行自蒸馏教师提供 token 级别监督；4) 使用负向 rubrics 引导后续 rollout 生成避免重复失败模式。

Result: 在不同模型骨干网络上，RISED 实现了最高的平均通过率，且在每个单独环境中排名第一或第二。Rubric 分析进一步揭示了伴随这些性能提升的行为变化。

Conclusion: 通过将 rubrics 从奖励信号扩展到在线数据选择和策略监督，RISED 成功解决了多环境强化学习中跨环境关系学习和组内奖励对比的问题，为构建通用智能体提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Training a single LLM agent jointly across diverse interactive environments has attracted increasing attention as a route to generalist agents. Existing curriculum and data-selection strategies often allocate training at the environment level or prioritize local reward-based signals, without explicitly considering relationships between current rollouts across environments for prompt-group selection. Meanwhile, as environments are learned at different rates, all-failure and all-success rollout groups can coexist within a batch, leaving those data without group-relative reward signals. Both challenges highlight limitations of relying solely on scalar rewards in multi-environment RL: they provide limited information about cross-environment relationships and no within-group reward contrast when rewards are identical. This motivates richer textual feedback, such as rubrics describing rollout behaviours, to guide learning. Beyond rubrics' usage as reward, we repurpose rubrics to guide both online data selection and policy supervision. An LLM judge tags each rollout using a predefined rubric vocabulary shared across environments. The resulting profiles guide the selection of data that aligns with the overall behavioural composition of the mixed-environment batch while limiting overlap with already-selected data. Available positive rubrics (describing desired behaviours) provide privileged context for an on-policy self-distillation teacher, supplying additional token-level supervision, while negative rubrics (describing undesired behaviours) guide subsequent rollout generation away from recurring failure modes. Together, these components form RISED. Across model backbones, RISED achieves the highest mean pass rate across environments and ranks first or second in every individual environment. Rubric-based analysis of RISED can further characterize the behavioural changes accompanying these gains.

</details>


### [234] [Evaluating LLM-Generated Preference Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01000)
*Fan Huang,Minsuk Kim,C. Tyler Diggans,Filippo Radicchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了大型语言模型(LLM)作为概率生成器时产生的偏好分布可靠性。研究涵盖9个开源模型、3个选择领域（航空旅行、餐厅、消费品），发现LLM虽具自一致性，但不同模型间存在显著差异，挑战了"足够能力的LLM能产生相似偏好分布"的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM作为模拟、合成数据生成和决策支持工具时，其生成的概率分布的结构和可靠性尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 系统分析LLM生成的航空旅行、餐厅和消费品偏好分布，通过重复采样测试自一致性，跨模型家族和规模比较一致性，测试温度变化、贪婪解码、提示和排序扰动下的鲁棒性。

Result: 所有模型表现出自一致性，最可能结果快速稳定；但跨模型家族和规模存在显著差异，即使最可能结果也缺乏共识；这些模式在温度变化、贪婪解码、提示和排序扰动下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 结果受模型选择的影响大于提示措辞的影响，挑战了"足够能力的LLM可作为调查对象替代品产生相似偏好分布"的常见假设。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as probabilistic generators for simulation, synthetic data generation, and decision support in settings where real-world data are unavailable. Yet, the structure and reliability of the distributions they produce remain understudied. Here, we systematically analyze LLM-generated distributions of preferences for air travel, restaurants, and consumer products. Encouragingly, all models considered in our analysis exhibit self-coherence, with the most probable outcomes stabilizing rapidly under repeated sampling. At the same time, we observe substantial discordance across both model families and scales, with little consensus even among their most probable outcomes. These patterns hold across nine open-weight models, three choice domains, and show robustness under temperature changes, greedy decoding, and perturbations of prompt and ordering. Our findings indicate that outcomes are influenced more by the choice of model than by the wording of the prompt, challenging the common assumption that sufficiently capable LLMs produce similar preference distributions when used as stand-ins for survey respondents.

</details>


### [235] [Calibration-risk routing for controlled world-model adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01001)
*Yifan Zhang,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对模拟器到目标环境的迁移问题，提出了模型修正世界模型(MC-WM)方法，将目标数据分为拟合、选择和校准三个不重叠的分区，部署标准化校准风险较低的模型族，并通过学习到的置信度信号和确定性有效性谓词来加权一步想象策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于模型的强化学习中，模拟器与目标环境之间的迁移会导致模型选择问题：当目标数据有限时，修正模拟器和直接拟合目标环境都可能失败。

Method: MC-WM方法将初始目标数据划分为互不重叠的拟合集、选择集和校准集，部署具有较低标准化校准风险的模型族。利用学习到的置信度信号和确定性有效性谓词对一步想象的策略更新进行加权，无需重写物理奖励函数。

Result: 在三个受控的MuJoCo接触式多关节动力学迁移环境中评估了540个独立报告的运行单元，其中一个精确路由单元在部署前工件门后重复运行，共计541次完成执行。

Conclusion: 该方法通过分离数据用途和基于校准风险选择模型，能够在模拟器到目标迁移场景下更有效地利用有限的目标数据。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) can exploit simulated experience, but a simulator-to-target shift creates a model-selection problem: correcting the simulator and fitting the target directly can each fail under limited target data. We introduce the Model-Corrected World Model (MC-WM), which separates initial target data into disjoint fit, selection, and calibration partitions and deploys the family with lower standardized calibration risk. A learned confidence signal and deterministic validity predicates weight one-step imagined policy updates without rewriting physical rewards. We evaluate 540 unique reported run cells across three controlled Multi-Joint dynamics with Contact (MuJoCo) shifts; one exact-routing cell was repeated after a pre-deployment artifact gate, giving 541 completed executions.

</details>


### [236] [What Can Analogy Tell Us About Artificial Consciousness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01002)
*Keith J. Holyoak,Martin M. Monti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个因果框架，用于评估关于非人类实体（如AI）意识的证据类比。核心区分在于产生意识的因素相似性与下游行为/认知效应的相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于主观体验只能通过第一人称直接获取，对其他实体意识的判断依赖于类比推理。随着人工智能的发展，需要一个系统性的方法来评估AI意识的可能性。

Method: 开发因果框架，对源-目标相似性进行因果相关性加权，同时考虑未知原因、禁用差异和通往意识的替代路径。

Result: 该框架解释为何随着与人类因果距离增加，类比支持减弱；对当代AI而言，行为相似性仅提供有限的意识证据，因为相关因果对应尚未建立。

Conclusion: 该框架澄清了何种证据可以加强人工意识的声称，为评估AI意识提供了更严谨的方法论基础。

Abstract: Who or what is conscious? Because subjective experience is directly accessible only in the first person, judgments about consciousness in other entities depend partly on analogy. Historically, such inferences have focused on nonhuman animals, but advances in artificial intelligence have raised the possibility of conscious AI. Here we develop a causal framework for evaluating such evidential analogies. The key distinction is between similarities in factors plausibly involved in generating consciousness and similarities in downstream behavioural or cognitive effects. Our framework weights source-target similarity by causal relevance while allowing for unknown causes, disabling differences and alternative routes to consciousness. Applied to biological systems, it explains why analogical support generally weakens with increasing causal distance from humans. Applied to contemporary AI, it suggests that behavioural similarity provides only limited evidence for consciousness because relevant causal correspondences remain poorly established. The framework also clarifies what evidence would strengthen claims of artificial consciousness.

</details>


### [237] [Beyond Answer Confidence: A Controlled Audit of Self-Knowledge in a Black-Box Decision Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01006)
*Sharath M Shankaranarayana,Davor Runje,Jan Jannink*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究审计了决策模型Jev的置信度是否真正反映"缺失知识"还是仅仅反映"随机不确定性"。研究发现，虽然置信度在熟悉任务上经过校准后可用，但在面对未知信息时过度自信，且简单的重新校准无法解决此问题。直接询问模型"是否知道答案"比置信度更能识别知识边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前决策模型的概率输出经过校准后可用，但校准无法解决一个根本问题：低置信度究竟代表"随机不确定性"还是"缺失知识"？模型在其知识边界之外的表现是否可信？这些问题对于模型的实际部署至关重要。

Method: 在15个以上公开数据集和6个生成任务家族上审计Jev模型，使用配对干预实验改变给定项目的相关信息，并比较置信度与准确率的差异。

Result: Jev的置信度在熟悉的封闭选择任务上经过校准，但无法作为缺失知识的指标：在无答案相关信息时仍给显著选项分配高达0.80的概率；在超出观察到的知识边界的新闻上，置信度超过准确率0.21-0.33，且使用早期月份数据的重新校准无法弥合这一差距。直接询问"结果是否已确定"（AUROC 1.00）和"证据是否充分"（0.95 vs 同一项目置信度0.85）能提供更清晰的判断。询问"Jev是否知道答案"可识别虚假实体和边界后新闻（0.91），但使用真实姓名或移除日期后无优势。

Conclusion: 黑盒知识审计需要对表面线索进行显式控制。直接询问模型是否知道答案比依赖置信度更可靠，但需注意表面特征对模型输出的影响。

Abstract: Decision models return probabilities intended for routing, abstention and automated action. Calibration makes those probabilities useful on average, but does not establish whether low confidence reflects chance or missing knowledge, nor whether confidence falls when a model moves beyond what it knows. We audit this distinction in Jev, a decision model, with over 15 public datasets and 6 generated task families, with paired interventions that vary the information supplied for a fixed item. Jev's confidence is calibrated on familiar closed-choice tasks but fails as an indicator of missing knowledge: with no answer-relevant information it assigns up to 0.80 to a salient option, and on news beyond an observed knowledge boundary it exceeds accuracy by 0.21--0.33, a gap that recalibration on earlier months does not close. Targeted yes/no questions give sharper readouts of the case: whether an outcome is settled (AUROC 1.00) and whether the evidence suffices (0.95, against 0.85 for confidence on the same items). Asking whether Jev knows the answer appears to flag fabricated entities and post-boundary news (0.91), but with realistic names or with dates removed it shows no advantage over answer uncertainty. Black-box knowledge audits therefore need explicit controls for surface cues. Code: https://github.com/Syntheme/beyond-answer-confidence.

</details>


### [238] [From Discovery to Decision: Finite-Budget Recoverability in LLM Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01014)
*Shaoang Li,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM推理中投票机制的"发现-决策差距"问题。当在固定调用预算下采样多个响应时，正确答案可能被过早发现但因剩余调用不足而无法成为最终胜者。作者通过"实现投票状态"和剩余调用预算来刻画这一差距，推导了精确的恢复性阈值，证明了候选集只能扩展而可达终点的胜者只能收缩，并给出了iid响应定律下的精确有限视界概率。研究还表明合并错误答案身份无法提升多数投票准确率，而重新分配错误答案概率的效果取决于实现的投票状态。"单例可达性"产生了一种无金的精确锁定证书。实验表明，大多数被发现但未选择的正确答案只在发现后失去可达性，输入排列使原始多数投票准确率提升21.1个百分点，精确锁定在16次调用预算下节省28-30%的调用同时保持所有固定预算输出的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在测试时扩展和集成推理中，对多个LLM响应进行投票是一种常见做法。收集更多响应可以扩展候选池并增加发现正确答案的机会。但在固定调用预算下，发现的答案仍需在剩余调用中积累足够支持才能成为最终多数胜者，这就产生了"发现-决策差距"问题。作者旨在量化这一差距并寻找优化策略。

Method: 1. 通过"实现投票状态"和剩余调用预算来形式化发现-决策差距；2. 推导精确的恢复性阈值；3. 证明采样过程中候选集只能扩展而可达终点胜者只能收缩；4. 在指定iid响应定律下给出精确有限视界端点概率；5. 分析合并错误答案身份的影响；6. 提出基于单例可达性的"精确锁定"证书方法；7. 在Word16数据集上验证方法效果。

Result: 理论结果：(1)候选集在采样过程中只能扩展，可达终点胜者只能收缩；(2)合并错误答案身份保持单次调用正确性但无法提升多数投票准确率；(3)错误答案概率重分配的效果取决于实现的投票状态。实证结果：(1)大多数被发现但未选择的正确答案在发现后立即失去可达性；(2)输入排列在Word16上提升原始多数投票准确率21.1个百分点，同时单次调用正确性基本不变；(3)精确锁定在16次调用预算下节省28-30%的调用，同时保持所有固定预算输出的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文成功刻画了LLM集成推理中发现的正确答案与被选中之间的理论差距，推导了精确的恢复性条件和概率界。提出的精确锁定方法能在保证输出正确性的前提下显著节省计算资源，为测试时扩展提供了有效的资源分配策略。关键发现是大多数正确答案是在已经失去可达性后才发现的，这解释了为什么简单的多数投票往往不是最优的。

Abstract: Voting over multiple LLM responses is a common primitive in test-time scaling and ensemble inference. Collecting more responses can expand the candidate pool and increase the chance that a correct answer is discovered. Under a fixed call budget, a discovered answer still needs to accumulate enough support within the remaining calls to become the final plurality winner, creating a discovery-to-decision gap. In this work, we characterize this gap through the realized vote state and remaining call budget. We derive a sharp recoverability threshold and show that, as sampling proceeds, the observed candidate set can only expand while the set of reachable endpoint winners can only contract, inducing a candidate-level conversion window. Under a specified iid response law, the same state yields exact finite-horizon endpoint probabilities. We further show that merging wrong-answer identities preserves single-call correctness and cannot improve plurality accuracy, and that the effect of redistributing wrong-answer probability depends on the realized vote state. Singleton reachability yields a gold-free exact locking certificate. For a known answer universe, its first trigger is the earliest prefix at which all admissible continuations yield the same fixed-budget output. Empirically, most discovered-but-unselected correct answers lose reachability only after discovery. In a controlled Word16 study, input permutation improves raw-plurality accuracy by 21.1 points with essentially unchanged single-call correctness. Exact locking saves 28-30% of calls at a 16-call budget while preserving every fixed-budget output.

</details>


### [239] [Pay for the Fault, Not the Flow: Label-Free In-Flow Multi-Agent Workflow Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01017)
*Xuehang Guo,Haoyu Wang,Shengyu Chen,Zach Chen,Wei Cheng,Qingyun Wang,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InFlowOp是一个用于优化LLM多智能体工作流的框架，通过无标签成本来权衡智能体能力与任务需求的匹配度以及运行成本，在执行前双向确定任务分解粒度和智能体分配，执行中通过相同成本纠正故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建多智能体工作流面临挑战：任务分解粒度、智能体分配、是否创建新专业智能体等决策难以事先确定，工作流改进成本高，定位故障需要参考答案或训练评估器。

Method: 提出InFlowOp框架，用单一无标签成本定价每个决策，权衡智能体能力与任务需求的匹配度及运行成本。执行前双向确定任务分解粒度和智能体分配；执行中通过最小成本移动纠正故障。同时引入Braid基准测试。

Result: 在各种领域和骨干网络上，InFlowOp优于单智能体基线高达+11.97%，使用流内优化达到+9.64%。

Conclusion: InFlowOp通过统一的成本度量有效解决了多智能体工作流的任务分解和智能体分配问题，实现了工作流构建和执行阶段的联合优化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly construct multi-agent workflows that decompose a complex task and assign specialist agents from a pool. However, building such a workflow well remains challenging: how finely to divide the task, which agent to trust with each subtask, and when to create a new specialist are all critical decisions a workflow constructor needs to settle up front. Thus, whether each subtask succeeds remains unknown until the workflow runs. Yet, improving a workflow is costly. Locating a fault usually requires a reference answer, a graded outcome, or a trained assessor, and the fix is applied to the whole workflow through re-execution, re-search, or retraining. We propose InFlowOp, which prices every decision in one label-free cost that weighs how well an agent's competence meets what a subtask demands against how much that agent takes to run. Before execution, InFlowOp bidirectionally determines the granularity of task decomposition and agent assignment following from the cost rather than from a fixed template. During execution, InFlowOp corrects a fault with the cheapest move via the same cost that serves the workflow both as it is built and as it runs. Facing the workflow-level evaluation challenge, we introduce Braid, a benchmark whose tasks require multi-agent coordination beyond single-agent capability. Across various domains and backbones, InFlowOp outperforms single agent baselines by up to $+11.97\%$, achieving $+9.64\%$ with in-flow optimization. Our project page: https://xhguo7.github.io/InFlowOp/.

</details>


### [240] [Beyond Final Accuracy: Auditing Communication in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01042)
*Shixuan Li,Wei Yang,Peiyu Zhang,Anzhe Cheng,Heng Ping,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ICR框架，通过将通信定义为独立推理后的答案修正来评估多智能体通信效果，解决了现有评估中难以区分有效通信、智能体架构和额外推理贡献的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体通信研究中，系统性能提升的来源不明确：究竟是有效通信、智能体架构还是额外推理的作用？由于通信方法通常在其设计的目标系统中评估，这些因素难以分离。此外，最终准确率将修正的错误和被破坏的答案混为一谈，掩盖了通信如何改变决策。

Method: 引入独立-通信-修正(ICR)受控框架：将通信定义为独立推理后的答案修正，固定初始推理轨迹，测量修正和保留（取决于智能体的初始正确性），并使用无消息修正控制来量化超越额外推理的增益。在四个推理基准上审计文本和潜在通信。

Result: 相似 aggregate 准确率可能掩盖显著不同的修正行为。与仅传输答案相比，充分推理在所有四个基准上增加修正同时减少保留，丰富消息放大了有益和有害影响。MedQA和GPQA-D上的接收者策略比较显示，结构化验证策略使每个通道转向更大保留和更低修正，而其对选择性的影响因通道和任务而异。

Conclusion: 将通信质量视为通道的内在属性是错误的。ICR将评估重点重新置于选择性修正上，提供了一个统一框架来检验消息内容和接收者策略如何共同产生收益和危害。

Abstract: Multi-agent communication aims to help agents benefit from one another's information. Yet improvements in system performance leave a fundamental ambiguity: do they reflect effective communication, a favorable agent architecture, or simply additional reasoning? Because communication methods are commonly evaluated within the systems they were designed for, these factors are difficult to disentangle. Final accuracy further merges corrected errors and corrupted answers into a single outcome, obscuring how communication changes decisions. We introduce Independent--Communicate--Revise (ICR), a controlled framework that evaluates communication as answer revision following independent reasoning. ICR fixes initial reasoning trajectories, measures correction and preservation conditional on both agents' initial correctness, and uses a no-message revision control to quantify gains beyond additional reasoning. Across four reasoning benchmarks, our audit of textual and latent communication reveals that similar aggregate accuracy can conceal substantially different revision behaviors. Compared with transmitting answers alone, full reasoning increases correction while reducing preservation on all four benchmarks, so richer messages amplify beneficial and harmful influence alike. Receiver-policy comparisons on MedQA and GPQA-D further show that a structured verification policy shifts every channel toward greater preservation and lower correction, while its effect on selectivity varies across channels and tasks. These findings challenge treating communication quality as an intrinsic property of a channel. ICR therefore recenters evaluation on selective revision, providing a unified framework for examining how message content and receiver policies jointly produce benefits and harms.

</details>


### [241] [Empty Commitments: When Agents Promise What Their Runtime Cannot Deliver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01045)
*Jiaqi Tang,Lan Wei,Bingyu Shen,Boyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了聊天机器人中的"空承诺"概念——即代理在当前回合后无法执行的承诺，并提出了测量协议来评估不同持久性配置下的承诺履行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聊天机器人常做出如"我明天提醒你"这样的承诺，但由于配置限制，这些承诺往往无法被实现。现有研究未充分区分"空承诺"（因配置本身无法履行）与"破裂承诺"（需要后续轨迹才能判断）。

Method: 在承诺语义基础上定义空承诺，提出三种失败类型、工具可实现承诺的锚定条件及响应级结果分类。测量协议包含五个逐步增加持久性功能的实验设置，并区分隐式和显式环境。

Result: 建立了一套系统性的空承诺分析框架，包括概念定义、分类体系和测量方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为评估和改进聊天机器人的承诺履行能力提供了理论工具和实证方法，有助于设计更可靠的对话代理系统。

Abstract: A chatbot that says "I will remind you tomorrow" will not run again until the user writes. We call such a promise an empty commitment: a promise of an action after the current turn that nothing in the agent's tools or runtime can carry out. Unlike a broken promise, its emptiness follows from the agent's configuration alone; no later trajectory is needed. We define empty commitments on top of commitment semantics, with three failure types, an anchoring condition for promises that a tool could make real, and a response-level outcome taxonomy. We then describe a measurement protocol: follow-up requests run in five setups that add one persistence affordance at a time, with the environment either left implicit or stated.

</details>


### [242] [Network World Models as Environments for Algorithm Design on Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01048)
*Rishab Alagharu,Hongji Pu,Zeeshan Memon,Xinyuan Song,Yuntong Hu,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出动作条件网络世界模型(Action-conditioned Network World Model)，用于学习干预下网络扩散动态，在算法设计循环中作为快速评估器，可使rollout速度提升14.5倍，在141个设置中的138个达到或超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界应用中，许多行动的效果并非立即显现（如节点免疫、活动推广等），需要预测后续展开的结果。传统方法依赖蒙特卡洛模拟评估候选行动，成本过高成为瓶颈。

Method: 提出动作条件网络世界模型，学习干预下网络扩散动态；构建编码智能体设计循环，利用完整rollout反馈、动作级信用和反事实探测来优化算法。

Result: 在8个网络任务和5个扩散模型上，所设计算法在141个设置中的138个达到或超越最强报告基线；相比蒙特卡洛模拟，rollout速度提升最高达14.5倍。

Conclusion: 动作条件世界模型可有效加速网络干预任务中的算法评估，为自动化算法设计提供高效反馈机制，具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: World models, which simulate an environment and predict how it changes under actions, are increasingly used in real-world applications such as robotics. Complex systems call for the same tool because the effect of an action is not immediate. Seeding nodes for a campaign, or immunizing nodes against an epidemic, changes little on its own; what matters is the outcome that unfolds over the steps that follow. Designing an algorithm that selects such actions to maximize expected performance on a task is inherently iterative, and every candidate must be scored by the outcome it produces. Obtaining that outcome has relied on simulation, whose cost becomes a bottleneck when candidates are evaluated over many sampled trajectories. We propose an action-conditioned Network World Model that learns a network's diffusion dynamics under interventions over time, applies each action to the network, and predicts the outcome that follows. It serves as a fast evaluator inside an algorithm design loop in which a coding agent designs and refines executable algorithms using feedback from full rollouts, action-level credit, and counterfactual probes over alternative interventions. Across eight network tasks and five diffusion models, the designed algorithms match or exceed the strongest reported baseline in 138 of 141 settings while enabling up to 14.5 times faster rollouts than Monte Carlo simulation. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [243] [Improving Math Reasoning through Value-guided Informative Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01080)
*Shaohuai Liu,Yuning Wu,Haoran Liu,Enzo Jia,Devin Chen,Kai Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APIVIS是一种在训练时间内将有限预算Gumbel搜索适应于分块级数学推理的框架，通过结合直接响应和搜索响应，并选择性监督搜索改进的token来改进RLVR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在RLVR推展中引入搜索以增加轨迹多样性，但多样性本身不能确保搜索诱导的推展策略优于当前策略，存在改进空白。

Method: APIVIS框架：1)将有限预算Gumbel搜索适应于分块级数学推理；2)在每个推展组内结合直接响应和搜索响应，使搜索发现的改进产生信息性相对奖励；3)对搜索改进的token应用选择性监督，当统一组奖励使GRPO失效时保留学习信号。

Result: 理论证明：精确值引导选择可提高每个搜索状态的期望验证器奖励，并扩展到完整推展策略，在有界值估计误差下有相应的近似保证。实验表明在数学推理基准和不同模型规模上均优于竞争性搜索方法。

Conclusion: APIVIS通过搜索增强的RLVR训练框架成功解决了搜索诱导改进的信号问题，理论保证与实验验证均表明其可显著提升大型语言模型的数学推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved the mathematical reasoning capabilities of large language models. Recent work introduces search into RLVR rollouts to increase trajectory diversity, but diversity alone does not ensure that the search-induced rollout policy improves upon the current policy. To address this gap, we propose APIVIS, a training-time framework that adapts finite-budget Gumbel search to chunk-level mathematical reasoning. APIVIS combines direct and searched responses within each rollout group, allowing improvements found by search to produce informative relative rewards. It further applies selective supervision to search-improved tokens, preserving a learning signal when uniform group rewards render GRPO ineffective. We show that exact value-guided selection improves the expected verifier reward at each searched state and that this guarantee extends to the complete rollout policy, with a corresponding approximate guarantee under bounded value-estimation error. Experiments on widely recognized mathematical reasoning benchmarks and different model scales demonstrate substantial improvements over competitive search-based methods, validating the effectiveness of APIVIS.

</details>


### [244] [YouRA: A Persistent-State Architecture for Evidence-Traceable Autonomous Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01097)
*Yoonkyu Woo,Woojin Lee,Jin-Xia Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: YouRA是一种状态化、可证据追溯的自主研究架构，通过VSA、独立控制器和状态化反思三个组件解决研究代理中声称与实验结果不一致的问题，在MLR-Bench上取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端研究代理虽能生成完整科学论文，但手稿声称与执行实验结果常不一致。这是一个结构性问题：研究状态、失败历史和声称-证据对齐未在长视距管道中保持为持久、可验证的状态。

Method: 集成了三个核心组件：(1)验证状态架构(VSA)追踪假设、门控和证据指针；(2)独立控制器将状态和反思记录转化为生命周期、恢复和辩论/审查控制；(3)状态化反思将失败记录为结构化教训，并通过有界修复、重设计或重置路由恢复。使用MLR-Bench的幻觉分类法进行自动诊断。

Result: 在MLR-Bench的预定义十任务端到端子集上，YouRA在所有三个匹配的主干网络上在Overall指标上均优于ML-Agent和AI Scientist V2。自动化诊断显示事实性失败类型更少，基于真实数据的输出更多。

Conclusion: YouRA通过状态化、可验证的研究框架解决了端到端代理中声称与实验结果不一致的结构性问题。该架构在MLR-Bench上优于MLR-Agent和AI Scientist V2，各组件的消融实验证实了其 separable contributions，移除任一核心组件都会导致性能下降。

Abstract: End-to-end research agents can now produce complete scientific papers, yet manuscript claims often diverge from executed experiments. This gap is structural: research state, failure histories, and claim-evidence alignment are not maintained as persistent, verifiable state across long-horizon pipelines. We present YouRA (Your Research Agent), an architecture for stateful, evidence-traceable autonomous research. YouRA preserves research state, execution evidence, and failure history across the research trajectory by integrating three components: a Verification State Architecture (VSA) that tracks hypotheses, gates, and evidence pointers; an Independent Controller that turns state and reflection records into lifecycle, recovery, and debate/review control while separating control from execution; and Stateful Reflection that logs failures as structured lessons and routes recovery through bounded repair, redesign, or reset. On MLR-Bench's predefined ten-task end-to-end subset, YouRA improves over both MLR-Agent and AI Scientist V2 on scalar Overall across all three matched backbones. An automated diagnostic using MLR-Bench's hallucination taxonomy reports intersection/union counts for four fact-based failure types, and data-provenance diagnostic shows more real-data-based outputs. Ablating each of the four components (the VSA, the Independent Controller, MCP tool access, and reflection-guided recovery) supports their separable contributions. Removing either core-state component drops YouRA below the full system. Code: https://github.com/PrayPrey/Your-Research-Agent.

</details>


### [245] [Beyond State-of-the-Art: Standardising Environmental Impact Metrics for AI Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01116)
*Lachlan McGinness,Dan Pagendam,Robert Offner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 随着大型语言模型(LLM)的普及,其碳排放问题日益严重,但机器学习社区缺乏标准化的碳排放核算实践。该论文通过对NeurIPS 2025的5,285篇论文进行自动化文献综述,发现环境影响的报告几乎不存在。为推动可持续AI发展,定义了评估模型训练效率的标准化可持续性指标,并提供了估计LLM推理碳成本的简单启发式方法。开发了carbonbenchmark工具用于跟踪和报告排放。为解决追求边际准确率提升而造成不成比例的环境成本问题,正式提出了"最小模型完成工作"(SMAJ)框架,挑战该领域在追求传统"最先进"(SotA)准确率的同时,优先考虑计算效率和环境责任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM的能力和普及度不断增长,其环境足迹也在增加。尽管有负责任AI的呼吁,但机器学习社区缺乏标准化的碳排放核算实践。NeurIPS 2025的5,285篇论文中几乎没有环境影响的报告,这促使研究者需要推动可持续AI的发展。

Method: 1. 对NeurIPS 2025的5,285篇论文进行自动化文献综述;2. 定义标准化可持续性指标用于评估模型训练效率;3. 提出估计LLM推理碳成本的启发式方法;4. 开发carbonbenchmark软件工具;5. 提出SMAJ(最小模型完成工作)框架。

Result: 发现环境影响的报告在顶级会议论文中几乎不存在。定义了评估模型训练效率的标准化可持续性指标,开发了carbonbenchmark工具,并提出SMAJ框架挑战传统仅追求SotA准确率的做法。

Conclusion: 论文提出了系统性的可持续AI实践方案:标准化可持续性指标、碳排放跟踪工具以及SMAJ框架,为推动AI领域向更环保负责的方向发展提供了重要基础。

Abstract: As the capabilities and ubiquity of Large Language Models (LLMs) grow, so does their environmental footprint. Despite calls for responsible AI, the machine learning community lacks standardised practices for carbon accounting. Our automated literature review of the 5,285 papers accepted to NeurIPS 2025 reveals that reporting of environmental impact is nearly non-existent. To catalyse a shift toward sustainable AI, we define standardised sustainability metrics for evaluating model training efficiency, accompanied by simple heuristics to estimate the carbon cost of LLM inference. We implement these metrics in carbonbenchmark, a drop-in software solution for tracking and reporting emissions.
  Finally, to combat the pursuit of marginal accuracy gains at disproportionate environmental costs, we formalise the `Smallest Model that Achieves the Job' (SMAJ), a framework which challenges the field to prioritise computational efficiency and environmental accountability alongside traditional `State-of-the-Art' (SotA) accuracy.

</details>


### [246] [AbsorbEvo: An Agentic Framework for Autonomous Inverse Design of Microwave Absorbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01119)
*Zhicheng Feng,Yubo Zhao,Xuefeng Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AbsorbEvo是一个基于智能体的自主逆设计框架,能将自然语言性能目标转换为经全波仿真验证的微波吸收器设计。该系统整合语言推理、基于物理的预测和历史反馈,在大规模语言模型指导下生成候选设计,并通过低成本预测模型作为物理先验进行排序筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计高性能微波吸收器需要电磁理论、材料科学和仿真编程方面的专业知识,且优化过程耗时。现有方法缺乏将自然语言性能目标直接转换为可验证设计的自动化能力。

Method: 提出候选演化策略,整合语言推理、基于物理的预测和历史反馈。大语言模型根据任务目标和计算历史提出参数调整方向和幅度;结合定向增量与全局采样生成候选设计;使用低成本预测模型作为物理先验对候选进行排序;仅对高排名设计进行全波仿真;通过物理有效性检查的结果用于评估性能并指导后续搜索;将训练任务经验提炼为文本技能并独立验证后再用于新任务。

Result: 在AbsorbBench-36任务上,相同提议预算下,AbsorbEvo任务成功率达79.17%,而通用智能体为25.00%,随机搜索为12.50%。平均最佳覆盖率为0.7816,后两者分别为0.6434和0.6448。

Conclusion: AbsorbEvo通过将语言推理和基于物理的反馈整合到设计决策中,为自然语言驱动的微波吸收器自主逆设计提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Designing high-performance microwave absorbers requires specialized expertise in electromagnetic theory, materials science and simulation programming, and entails time-consuming optimization. Here, we present AbsorbEvo, an agentic framework for autonomous inverse design that translates natural-language performance objectives into designs verified by full-wave simulations. Its candidate evolution strategy integrates language reasoning, physics-based prediction and historical feedback. A large language model proposes the directions and magnitudes of parameter adjustments based on task objectives and computational history. The system combines directed increments with global sampling to generate candidates and uses a low-cost predictive model as a physics prior to rank them. Only high-ranking designs undergo full-wave simulation. Results passing physical validity checks are used to evaluate performance and guide subsequent search. Experience from training tasks is further distilled into textual skills, which are independently validated before use in new tasks. Under identical proposal budgets on held-out AbsorbBench-36 tasks, AbsorbEvo achieved a task success rate of 79.17%, versus 25.00% for a generic agent and 12.50% for random search. Its mean best coverage was 0.7816, compared with 0.6434 and 0.6448, respectively. By integrating language reasoning and physics-based feedback into design decisions, AbsorbEvo provides a methodological foundation for natural-language-driven autonomous inverse design of microwave absorbers.

</details>


### [247] [CortexBridge: Cortical Alignment of EEG Montages for Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01124)
*Jiazhen Hong,Xiaotian Zhou,Zihao Ding,Kailong Wang,Yu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CortexBridge是一个轻量级适配器,用于解决脑电图(EEG)基础模型在不同电极布局下的迁移困难问题,通过将任意脑电montage映射到共享的皮层潜在空间来改进脑机接口分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型通常使用固定的通道词汇表或有限的montage进行预训练,当电极布局发生变化时,模型迁移变得困难。

Method: CortexBridge结合EEG特征、电极坐标和 atlas 坐标,将任意montage映射到共享的皮层潜在空间。在三个冻结的基础模型(EEGPT、LaBraM、CBraMod)和五个来自MOABB的BCI数据集上进行了评估。

Result: 在15次评估中提升了13次。平衡准确率平均提升:EEGPT提升0.80%、LaBraM提升0.70%、CBraMod提升3.26%。在12类稳态视觉诱发电位(SSVEP)分类中最大提升13.02%。可视化显示SSVEP在Yeo视觉网络中表现出更集中的表征,而听觉P300则不同。

Conclusion: 皮层对齐是一种可学习的、解剖学上合理的路由机制,能够将异构EEG montage连接到预训练的基础模型。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models are often pretrained with a fixed channel vocabulary or a limited set of montages, making transfer difficult when electrode layouts change. We propose CortexBridge, a lightweight adapter that combines EEG features with electrode and atlas coordinates to map arbitrary montages into a shared cortical latent space. Evaluated with three frozen foundation models on five brain-computer interface (BCI) datasets from the Mother of All BCI Benchmarks (MOABB), CortexBridge improves performance in 13 of 15 evaluations. The gains in balanced accuracy average 0.80% for EEGPT, 0.70% for LaBraM, and 3.26% for CBraMod, with a maximum gain of 13.02% on 12-class steady-state visual evoked potential (SSVEP) classification. Visualizations of the learned atlas representations reveal task-dependent spatial patterns, with SSVEP showing a more concentrated representation in the Yeo Visual network than auditory P300. These results establish cortical alignment as a learnable and anatomically grounded routing mechanism from heterogeneous EEG montages to pretrained foundation models.

</details>


### [248] [Grounding Large Language Models in DSGE Simulators for Policy Generation and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01128)
*Aditya Dubey,Namah Gupta,Vinti Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试指令微调的大型语言模型在六个DSGE宏观经济学模拟器中的政策决策能力，通过将模型置于经济仿真环境中让其选择政策行动并观察经济后果，从而评估模型是否能产生与经济动态一致的政策响应，而非仅仅听起来合理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可以生成听起来合理的经济政策响应，但这并不代表其行动与经济动态一致。现有的语言模型评估主要依赖人类判断或静态测试，无法验证模型在动态经济环境中的真实政策效果。

Method: 构建六种Snowdrop支持的DSGE模拟器，实现通用Python接口支持重复展开、持久冲击、状态克隆和滚动 horizon模拟。将语言模型置于模拟器中，每轮观察经济状况和经济话语变化，选择有界政策行动，接收下一状态和经济奖励。采用PPO（带学习值函数）和GRPO（无学习值函数的基线）进行训练和比较。

Result: 实验测试了方向性语义信号、奖励horizon、轨迹热身启动、跨模拟器迁移以及历史上锚定的疫情和货币政策冲击等多种因素。结果表明PPO能够通过广义优势估计将延迟奖励传播到早期token，而GRPO无法区分导致结果的具体轮次。

Conclusion: 通过模拟经济后果而非仅凭语言合理性来评判政策行动，可以更准确地评估语言模型在经济政策领域的能力。PPO在处理长期信用分配问题上优于GRPO，这为语言模型在宏观经济政策决策中的应用提供了重要参考。
